from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, HTTPException
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse, JSONResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
import logging

from backend.core.config import settings
from backend.core.logger import logger
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.services.voicevox_client import voicevox_client
from backend.core.agent import run_agent_stream, get_thread_history, get_all_threads, delete_thread_history

# App Initialization
app = FastAPI(title="AI Buddy API", version="2.0.0")

# CORS
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Exception Handler
@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    logger.error(f"Global Exception: {exc}", exc_info=True)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=500,
        content={"detail": "Internal Server Error. Check logs for details."},
    )

# Models
class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    message: str
    thread_id: str = "default_thread"
    mode: str = "normal"
    voice_id: Optional[str] = None
    image_data: Optional[str] = None # Base64

class ProfileUpdate(BaseModel):
    user_name: str
    pref_interests: str
    pref_style: str
    notes: str

# Endpoints

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    logger.info("Server Starting...")
    await memory_manager.init_database()
    logger.info("Database Initialized.")

@app.get("/")
async def root():
    return {"status": "ok", "version": "2.0.0", "backend": "active"}

@app.post("/stream_chat")
async def stream_chat(request: ChatRequest):
    logger.info(f"Chat Request: Thread={request.thread_id}, Msg={request.message[:20]}...")
    
    return StreamingResponse(
        run_agent_stream(
            input_text=request.message,
            thread_id=request.thread_id,
            voice_id=request.voice_id,
            image_data=request.image_data
        ),
        media_type="text/event-stream"
    )

@app.get("/history/{thread_id}")
async def get_history(thread_id: str):
    history = await get_thread_history(thread_id)
    return {"history": history}

@app.delete("/history/{thread_id}")
async def clear_history(thread_id: str):
    await delete_thread_history(thread_id)
    return {"status": "deleted"}

@app.get("/threads")
async def list_threads():
    threads = await get_all_threads()
    return {"threads": threads}

@app.get("/voicevox/speakers")
async def get_speakers():
    speakers = voicevox_client.get_speakers()
    return {"speakers": speakers}

@app.get("/profile")
async def get_profile():
    return memory_manager.load_profile()

@app.post("/profile")
async def update_profile(data: ProfileUpdate):
    profile = {
        "user_name": data.user_name,
        "preferences": {
            "interests": [i.strip() for i in data.pref_interests.split(",")],
            "style": data.pref_style
        },
        "notes": data.notes
    }
    if memory_manager.save_profile(profile):
        return {"status": "saved", "profile": profile}
    raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to save profile")

@app.get("/memories")
async def get_memories():
    episodes = await memory_manager.get_all_episodes()
    # Format for frontend
    formatted = []
    for ep in episodes:
        formatted.append(f"[{ep['timestamp']}] {ep['summary']} ({ep['category']})")
    
    # Also get triplets
    triplets = await memory_manager.get_triplets()
    for t in triplets:
        formatted.append(f"🕸️ {t['subject']} -[{t['predicate']}]-> {t['object']}")
        
    return {"memories": formatted}

@app.get("/config")
async def get_config():
    return {
        "llm_type": settings.LLM_TYPE,
        "mode_options": ["normal", "dev", "gaming"],
        "voice_id": settings.DEFAULT_VOICE_ID
    }

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import uvicorn
    uvicorn.run(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=8000)

import json

from backend.services.telemetry_logger import telemetry

def _ms() -> int:
    return int(time.time() * 1000)

from backend.services.tool_tag_parser import parse_tool_tags
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager

# --- Helpers ---
_AFFECT_RE = re.compile(r"^\[([A-Za-z0-9_\-]+)\]\s*(.*)$", re.DOTALL)

def _split_affect(text: str) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
    """
    "[neutral] hello" -> ("neutral", "hello")
    Otherwise -> (None, original)
    """
    if not text:
        return None, text
    m = _AFFECT_RE.match(text.strip())
    if not m:
        return None, text
    affect = m.group(1)
    body = m.group(2)
    return affect, body

    thread_id: str = "default_thread"
    voice_id: Optional[str] = "14"

# ---------- Helpers ----------

def _ensure_str(chunk: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(chunk, (bytes, bytearray)):
        return chunk.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    return str(chunk)


def _sse_json(obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return f"data: {json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\n"


async def _safe_stream_generator(
    input_text: str,
    thread_id: str,
    voice_id: str,
    image_data: Optional[str] = None,
    mode: str = "normal",
):
    """
    AgentのSSEを受け取り、フロントが JSON.parse 前提でも落ちないように整形する。
    - data: [DONE] を data: {"type":"done"} に変換
    - agentがdoneを出さず終了しても finally でdoneを送る
    - bytesにも対応
    """
    done_sent = False

    try:
        # ★ Lazy import（起動時クラッシュ/循環参照のリスクを下げる）
        from backend.core.agent import run_agent_stream
        from backend.services.chat_history_store import chat_history_store

        t0 = _ms()
        # 1) chat_received
        await telemetry.event(thread_id, "chat_received", {
            "user_len": len(input_text or ""),
            "has_image": bool(image_data),
        })
        
        # --- A-Layer: Log User Message ---
        current_turn_id = await chat_history_store.get_next_turn_id(thread_id)
        await chat_history_store.append_message(
            thread_id=thread_id,
            turn_id=current_turn_id,
            role="user",
            content={"text": input_text},
            attachments=[] 
        )
        
        accumulated_ai_text = ""
        first_token_sent = False
        
        # --- Tool Hiding State ---
        tool_hidden_mode = False
        first_tool_start_idx = None
        stream_buffer = "" # Buffer for handling split tags
        
        # 2) stream_started
        await telemetry.event(thread_id, "stream_started", {
            "mode": "stream_chat",
        })

        async for chunk in run_agent_stream(
            input_text=input_text,
            thread_id=thread_id,
            voice_id=voice_id,
            image_data=image_data,
            mode=mode,
        ):
            chunk_str = _ensure_str(chunk)
            line = chunk_str.strip()
            # logger.info(f"[DEBUG_ROUTER] recv: {line[:120]}") # DEBUG LOG -> INFO (Truncated)

            # SSEのdata行を読む
            if line.startswith("data: "):
                payload = line[6:].strip()

                # 1) 文字列DONEをJSON doneに変換
                if payload == "[DONE]":
                    # (Legacy flow logic remains if needed, or simplified)
                    pass

                # 2) Payload Parsing
                try:
                    data = json.loads(payload)
                    if isinstance(data, dict):
                        msg_type = data.get("type")
                        
                        # --- TEXT: Apply Filter ---
                        if msg_type == "text":
                            chunk_content = data.get("content", "")
                            
                            # (Telemetry for first token if needed)
                            accumulated_ai_text += chunk_content
                            if (not first_token_sent) and chunk_content:
                                first_token_sent = True
                                await telemetry.event(thread_id, "stream_first_token", {
                                    "ms_from_start": _ms() - t0
                                })

                            # --- ROBUST FILTER LOGIC ---
                            stream_buffer += chunk_content
                            
                            while stream_buffer:
                                if tool_hidden_mode:
                                    # In hidden mode, look for end tag "]]"
                                    end_idx = stream_buffer.find("]]")
                                    if end_idx != -1:
                                        # Found end tag -> Exit hidden mode
                                        # internal_content = stream_buffer[:end_idx+2]
                                        tool_hidden_mode = False
                                        stream_buffer = stream_buffer[end_idx+2:]
                                        # Continue loop to process remainder
                                    else:
                                        # End tag not found -> All hidden
                                        stream_buffer = "" # Clear buffer (consumed)
                                        break
                                else:
                                    # In visible mode, look for start tag "[["
                                    start_idx = stream_buffer.find("[[")
                                    if start_idx != -1:
                                        # Found start tag
                                        visible_part = stream_buffer[:start_idx]
                                        if visible_part:
                                            yield _sse_json({"type": "text", "content": visible_part})
                                        
                                        tool_hidden_mode = True
                                        stream_buffer = stream_buffer[start_idx:]
                                        # Continue loop to process hidden part
                                    else:
                                        # Start tag not found
                                        # Check for partial tag at end (e.g. "[")
                                        if stream_buffer.endswith("["):
                                            # Yield everything except last char
                                            visible_part = stream_buffer[:-1]
                                            if visible_part:
                                               yield _sse_json({"type": "text", "content": visible_part})
                                            stream_buffer = "[" # Keep partial tag in buffer
                                        else:
                                            # Safe to yield all
                                            yield _sse_json({"type": "text", "content": stream_buffer})
                                            stream_buffer = ""
                                        break
                            
                        # --- AUDIO / DONE / ERROR ---
                        elif msg_type in ["audio", "done", "error"]:
                            # Pass through as is
                            yield _sse_json(data)

                            if msg_type == "done":
                                done_sent = True
                                # (Cleanup logic...)
                                
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # REMOVED: yield chunk_str (Passthrough)

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"safe_stream_generator failed: {e}")
        # t0 は try ブロック内で定義されているため、ここで参照すると UnboundLocalError になる可能性がある。
        # なので初期値を None にしておき、存在確認する。
        duration = 0
        try:
             duration = _ms() - t0
        except: pass

        await telemetry.event(thread_id, "stream_error", {
            "error": str(e),
            "duration_ms": duration,
        })
        yield _sse_json({"type": "error", "content": str(e)})

    finally:
        if not done_sent:
            yield _sse_json({"type": "done"})


async def _collect_text_from_agent_stream(input_text: str, thread_id: str) -> str:
    """
    run_agent_stream(SSE) を内部で回し、type=text の content だけ結合して返す。
    [DONE] / {"type":"done"} 両対応。
    """
    full_text = ""

    try:
        from backend.core.agent import run_agent_stream

        async for chunk in run_agent_stream(input_text=input_text, thread_id=thread_id):
            chunk_str = _ensure_str(chunk).strip()

            if not chunk_str.startswith("data: "):
                continue

            payload = chunk_str[6:].strip()

            if payload == "[DONE]":
                break

            try:
                data = json.loads(payload)
            except Exception:
                continue

            if isinstance(data, dict):
                if data.get("type") == "text":
                    full_text += data.get("content", "")
                elif data.get("type") == "done":
                    break

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"collect_text_from_agent_stream failed: {e}")

    return full_text


# ---------- Endpoints ----------

@router.post("/stream_chat")
async def stream_chat(req: StreamChatRequest, request: Request):
    """
    UI用: JSONResponse (Phase 3: Plan A)
    - ストリーミングを廃止し、バックエンドで浄化済みの全文を返す。
    - フロントのSSE複雑性を排除し、漏洩リスクをゼロにする。
    """
    rid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    logger.info(f"[CHAT_START] rid={rid} from={request.client.host} mode={req.mode}")
    logger.info(f"📨 Chat Request: {req.text[:20]}... (Thread: {req.thread_id}) Mode: {req.mode}")

    from backend.core.agent import run_agent_non_stream
    
    result = await run_agent_non_stream(
        input_text=req.text,
        thread_id=req.thread_id,
        voice_id=req.voice_id,
        image_data=req.image_data,
        mode=req.mode
    )

    # 監査イベント
    try:
        await telemetry.event(req.thread_id, "chat_completed", {
            "rid": rid,
            "type": result.get("type"),
            "has_audio": bool(result.get("audio"))
        })
    except: pass

    return JSONResponse(result)


@router.post("/livestream_chat")
async def livestream_chat(req: LivestreamChatRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    配信コメント用: JSON一括返却
    - 内部でストリームを消費してテキスト結合
    """
    text = (req.message or "").strip()
    if not text:
        return {"ok": True, "thread_id": req.thread_id, "response": ""}

    resp_text = await _collect_text_from_agent_stream(text, req.thread_id)
    return {"ok": True, "thread_id": req.thread_id, "response": resp_text}


@router.get("/history/{thread_id}")
async def get_history(thread_id: str):
    """
    会話履歴取得
    - Agent側の get_thread_history を呼ぶ (Lazy Import)
    """
    try:
        from backend.core.agent import get_thread_history
        hist = await get_thread_history(thread_id)
        return {"ok": True, "history": hist}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"History fetch failed: {e}")
        return {"ok": True, "history": []}

# --- Profile API ---
class UserProfile(BaseModel):
    user_name: str
    bio: str = ""
    interests: List[str] = []
    traits: List[str] = []

@router.get("/profile")
async def get_user_profile():
    from backend.core.config import settings, PROJECT_ROOT
    json_path = PROJECT_ROOT / settings.USER_PROFILE_JSON
    
    if json_path.exists():
        try:
            with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"error": str(e)})
    else:
        # Return default structure if missing
        return {
            "user_name": "Origin",
            "bio": "",
            "interests": [],
            "traits": []
        }

@router.post("/profile")
async def update_user_profile(profile: UserProfile):
    from backend.core.config import settings, PROJECT_ROOT
    json_path = PROJECT_ROOT / settings.USER_PROFILE_JSON
    
    # Load existing to preserve 'stats' or extra fields not in model
    existing = {}
    if json_path.exists():
        try:
            with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                existing = json.load(f)
        except: pass
        
    # Merge updates
    updated_data = {**existing, **profile.model_dump()}
    
    try:
        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(updated_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        return {"status": "success", "profile": updated_data}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"error": str(e)})


@router.post("/system/vision")
async def set_vision_status(req: VisionRequest):
    from backend.core.config import settings, PROJECT_ROOT
    
    json_path = PROJECT_ROOT / settings.PUBLIC_STATUS_JSON
    
    status = {}
    if json_path.exists():
        with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            try:
                status = json.load(f)
            except: pass
            
    status["vision_enabled"] = req.enabled
    
    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(status, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
    logger.info(f"👁️ Vision Status Changed: {req.enabled}")
    return {"status": "success", "vision_enabled": req.enabled}


========== backend\controllers\pending_memory_router.py ==========
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
import json
import sqlite3
import traceback

from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.services.telemetry_logger import telemetry
from backend.core.config import settings

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/pending_memories", tags=["Pending Memories"])

class PendingMemoryItem(BaseModel):
    id: int # event table id
    timestamp: str
    thread_id: str
    summary: str
    details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    confidence: float

class ReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    event_id: int
    confirm: str # "APPROVE" or "REJECT"
    tags_override: Optional[List[str]] = None
    note: Optional[str] = None

@router.get("", response_model=List[PendingMemoryItem])
def list_pending_memories(limit: int = 50):
    """B層の 'memory_pending' イベントを取得"""
    conn = None
    try:
        print(f"DEBUG: Connecting to {settings.TELEMETRY_DB_PATH}")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.TELEMETRY_DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # TODO: 将来的には approved/rejected 済みのものを除外するLEFT JOINなどを検討
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT id, ts as timestamp, thread_id, payload_json 
            FROM events 
            WHERE event_type = 'memory_pending' 
            ORDER BY id DESC 
            LIMIT ?
        """, (limit,))
        
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        results = []
        for row in rows:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(row["payload_json"])
                results.append({
                    "id": row["id"],
                    "timestamp": row["timestamp"],
                    "thread_id": row["thread_id"],
                    "summary": payload.get("summary", "(No Summary)"),
                    "details": payload,
                    "confidence": payload.get("importance", 0.0)
                })
            except:
                continue
        return results
    except Exception as e:
        print("ERROR in list_pending_memories:")
        print(traceback.format_exc())
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Internal Error: {str(e)}")
    finally:
        if conn:
            conn.close()

@router.post("/approve")
async def approve_memory(req: ReviewRequest):
    if req.confirm != "APPROVE":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Confirmation needed (APPROVE)")

    # 1. 元のイベントを取得
    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.TELEMETRY_DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT payload_json, thread_id FROM events WHERE id = ?", (req.event_id,))
    row = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Event not found")
        
    payload = json.loads(row[0])
    thread_id = row[1]
    
    summary = payload.get("summary")
    surprise = payload.get("surprise", 0.0)
    importance = payload.get("importance", 0.0)
    
    # 2. C層へ保存
    await memory_manager.register_thought(
        text=summary,
        surprise=surprise,
        confidence=importance,
        source="manual_approval",
        thread_id=thread_id,
        tags=req.tags_override or ["approved"]
    )
    
    # 3. 承認ログ記録
    await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_approved", {
        "ref_event_id": req.event_id,
        "summary": summary,
        "note": req.note
    })
    
    return {"ok": True}

@router.post("/reject")
async def reject_memory(req: ReviewRequest):
    if req.confirm != "REJECT":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Confirmation needed (REJECT)")
        
    # 記録だけ残す
    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.TELEMETRY_DB_PATH, check_same_thread=False)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT thread_id FROM events WHERE id = ?", (req.event_id,))
    row = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    
    thread_id = row[0] if row else "unknown"

    await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_rejected", {
        "ref_event_id": req.event_id,
        "note": req.note
    })
    
    return {"ok": True}


========== backend\core\__init__.py ==========


========== backend\core\agent.py ==========
import os
import re
import json
import uuid
import base64
import asyncio
import datetime
import time
import copy
import operator
from typing import Annotated, Sequence, TypedDict, List, Optional, Dict, Any
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import MemorySaver

def extract_json_from_text(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Extract the first JSON object from model output robustly."""
    if not text:
        return None

    # 1) Prefer fenced code block: ```json ... ```
    codeblock_patterns = [
        r"```json\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```",
        r"```\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```",
    ]
    for pat in codeblock_patterns:
        m = re.search(pat, text, flags=re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            candidate = m.group(1).strip()
            try:
                obj = json.loads(candidate)
                return obj if isinstance(obj, dict) else None
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass

    # 2) Scan for the first decodable JSON object in raw text
    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    for m in re.finditer(r"\{", text):
        start = m.start()
        try:
            obj, end = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
            if isinstance(obj, dict):
                return obj
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue

    return None

FORBIDDEN_STORY_WORDS = [
    "貢献", "創造", "生まれ", "作った", "作成", "間接的", "影響", "きっかけ",
    "記録されています", "確か", "〜ですね", "誕生日", "年齢",
]

def normalize_ja(s: str) -> str:
    s = s or ""
    s = s.lower()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", "", s)
    return s

def is_summary_grounded(summary: str, user_text: str) -> bool:
    """Check if the summary is based on the user's explicit input (grounded)."""
    if not summary:
        return False
    ns = normalize_ja(summary)
    nu = normalize_ja(user_text)

    # ストーリー/因果っぽいワードが入ってたら要注意（Direct保存は禁止）
    if any(w in summary for w in FORBIDDEN_STORY_WORDS):
        return False

    # 単純な根拠チェック：summaryの主要トークンがユーザー文に含まれるか
    tokens = set(re.findall(r"[一-龠ぁ-んァ-ンa-zA-Z0-9]{2,}", summary))
    tokens = {t for t in tokens if t not in {"です", "ます", "だよ", "よね", "ありがとう"}}

    if not tokens:
        return False

    hit = sum(1 for t in tokens if normalize_ja(t) in nu)
    ratio = hit / max(len(tokens), 1)

    # 7割以上根拠があればOK
    return ratio >= 0.7

# プロジェクトルートの定義
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

# LangChain / LangGraph
from langchain_core.messages import BaseMessage, HumanMessage, SystemMessage, AIMessage, ToolMessage
from langchain_core.prompts import ChatPromptTemplate
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END
from langgraph.graph.message import add_messages
from langgraph.prebuilt import ToolNode

# Internal Services
from backend.core.config import settings
from backend.core.logger import logger
from backend.services.tools import tools
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.services.voicevox_client import voicevox_client

# Phase 7/8 Modules
from backend.services.vision_adapter import vision_adapter
from backend.services.hearing_adapter import hearing_adapter
from backend.services.thought_engine import thought_engine
from backend.services.inner_monologue_logger import log_inner_thought

from backend.services.speech_sanitizer import finalize_output


def _log_task_exception(t: asyncio.Task):
    try:
        t.result()
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("background task failed")

# Optional Ollama for Vision
try:
    from langchain_ollama import ChatOllama
except ImportError:
    ChatOllama = None

# --- Global State for Throttling ---
_LAST_SENSE_TIME = 0
_SENSE_COOLDOWN = 10.0  # 感覚器を動かす最小間隔(秒)
_LAST_THOUGHT_CACHE = None # 直前の思考をキャッシュ

# TypedDict State
class AgentState(TypedDict, total=False):
    messages: Annotated[Sequence[BaseMessage], add_messages]
    has_user_text: bool

# --- LLM Factory ---
def get_llm():
    if settings.LLM_TYPE == "openai":
        return ChatOpenAI(
            api_key=settings.OPENAI_API_KEY,
            model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL,
            temperature=0.7, 
            base_url=settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL,
            model_kwargs={
                "frequency_penalty": 0.6,
                "presence_penalty": 0.2
            }
        )
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported LLM_TYPE: {settings.LLM_TYPE}")

llm = get_llm()
# llm_with_tools = llm.bind_tools(tools)
llm_with_tools = llm # Tools Disabled (Tag Mode)
tool_node = ToolNode(tools)

# --- Helper Functions ---
def _is_recent_duplicate(messages: List[BaseMessage], tool_name: str, tool_args: dict, lookback: int = 15) -> bool:
    if tool_name == "see_screen": return False
    count = 0
    for msg in reversed(messages):
        if count >= lookback: break
        if isinstance(msg, AIMessage) and msg.tool_calls:
            for tc in msg.tool_calls:
                if tc['name'] == tool_name and tc['args'] == tool_args:
                    logger.warning(f"[Anti-Loop] Blocked duplicate tool: {tool_name}")
                    return True
        count += 1
    return False

def _parse_raw_json_tool_call(content: str):
    pattern = r'\{"name":\s*"([\w_]+)",\s*"arguments":\s*(\{.*?\})\}'
    match = re.search(pattern, content, re.DOTALL)
    if match:
        return match.group(1), match.group(2), match.group(0)
    return None, None, None

def _is_tool_loop(llm_input: List[BaseMessage], name: str, args: dict) -> bool:
    if len(llm_input) >= 2 and isinstance(llm_input[-1], ToolMessage) and isinstance(llm_input[-2], AIMessage):
        prev_ai = llm_input[-2]
        if prev_ai.tool_calls:
            prev_call = prev_ai.tool_calls[0]
            if prev_call['name'] == name and prev_call['args'] == args:
                return True
    return False

# --- Graph Nodes (最適化版) ---

async def call_model(state: AgentState, config: dict = None):
    global _LAST_SENSE_TIME, _LAST_THOUGHT_CACHE
    
    messages = state["messages"]
    non_system_messages = [m for m in messages if not isinstance(m, SystemMessage)]
    
    # Mode Handling
    mode = "normal"
    if config and "configurable" in config:
        mode = config["configurable"].get("mode", "normal")

    # 1. Recall (Memory)
    memory_context = ""
    last_user_msg = ""
    if non_system_messages and isinstance(non_system_messages[-1], HumanMessage):
        last_user_msg = non_system_messages[-1].content
        if isinstance(last_user_msg, str) and last_user_msg.strip():
            relevant_memories = await memory_manager.recall_memories(last_user_msg)
            if relevant_memories:
                memory_text = "\n".join([
                    f"- {m['content']} (確信度: {m.get('confidence_tag', 'mid')})" 
                    for m in relevant_memories
                ])
                # Layer 2: Memory (Strictly Internal)
                memory_context = f"""
[LAYER 2: RECALLED MEMORIES (INTERNAL ONLY)]
The following memories are for context only.
DO NOT narrate them. DO NOT start sentences with "I remember...".
Reflect them in your attitude, not your speech.
---
{memory_text}
---
"""

    # 2. ★ Perception & Thought (Throttled + Mutability Protection)
    now = time.time()
    should_sense = (now - _LAST_SENSE_TIME > _SENSE_COOLDOWN) or (mode == "dev")
    
    # 内省の重さを制御
    # ★先輩AIのアドバイス: "起動直後は重すぎると思考が硬直する"
    # デフォルトはDeep Reflection（LLM使用）をOFFにし、直感的な反応を優先する。
    use_deep_reflection = False

    thought = {}
    
    # Check vision toggle
    vision_enabled = False
    try:
        from backend.core.config import PROJECT_ROOT
        with open(PROJECT_ROOT / settings.PUBLIC_STATUS_JSON, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            st = json.load(f)
            vision_enabled = st.get("vision_enabled", False)
    except: pass

    if should_sense:
        v_data = {"scene_summary": "(視界は閉ざされている)", "ocr_text": "", "user_activity": "unknown"}
        
        if vision_enabled:
            logger.info(f"👁️ Senses Active (Deep={use_deep_reflection})")
            v_data = vision_adapter.capture_and_summarize()
        else:
            # logger.info(f"🙈 Vision Disabled (Hearing Only)")
            pass

        # (A) 見る・聞く (Hearing is always active if sense is active)
        h_data = hearing_adapter.capture_and_summarize()
        
        # (B) 考える
        thought = await thought_engine.reflect(v_data, h_data, use_llm=use_deep_reflection)
        
        # Cache更新
        _LAST_SENSE_TIME = now
        _LAST_THOUGHT_CACHE = thought
        
        # (C) 心に刻む (Inner Log Only)
        log_inner_thought(thought)
    else:
        logger.info("⏩ Senses Skipped (Using Cache)")
        if _LAST_THOUGHT_CACHE:
            # ★ 修正: ミュータブル汚染防止のための deepcopy
            thought = copy.deepcopy(_LAST_THOUGHT_CACHE)
            # キャッシュであることを明示（ログ用に追記するが、deepcopyなので元データは汚染されない）
            thought["interpretation"] += " (Cached)"
        else:
            # キャッシュすらない初回などの場合
            thought = {"interpretation": "特になし", "inner_state": "Neutral", "inner_reflection": "..."}

    # (C-2) 物理的同期: ユーザー入力がある場合は強制的に対話モードへ
    has_user_text = bool(state.get("has_user_text", False))
    if has_user_text:
        thought["express"] = True
        if thought.get("intent") == "breathe":
            thought["intent"] = "support_quietly"

    current_time_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M JST")
    perception_log = f"""
<INTERNAL_LOG>
TIME: {current_time_str}
Observation: {thought.get('interpretation')}
Internal State: {thought.get('inner_state')}
Intent: {thought.get('intent')}
Inner Monologue: {thought.get('inner_reflection')}
</INTERNAL_LOG>
"""

    system_instruction = f"""
SYSTEM INSTRUCTION (HIGHEST PRIORITY):
- Output format:
  1) [LAYER 3: SPEECH] (human-facing, natural Japanese)
  2) Optional system tag lines at the end, each on its own line, starting with [[...]] followed by one-line strict JSON.
- If you output a system tag line, it must be complete on a single line: [[TAG_NAME]] {{strict one-line JSON}}. Do not split, wrap, or partially output brackets.
- Never output stray bracket characters like "]" or "[[" as standalone text.
- The information inside <INTERNAL_LOG> is for your private consideration ONLY.
- DO NOT output, narrate, or summarize anything from <INTERNAL_LOG> in your speech.
- Your speech must be natural as "Nia", and NEVER include raw JSON, braces {{}}, or internal keys like "summary" inside the speech.
- NEVER include layer tags like [LAYER 3: SPEECH] or prefixes like "Nia:" inside the speech content itself.
- If you output any system tags, you MUST still output at least one complete SPEECH sentence first.
- If silence is truly the best choice for the user's situation, output ONLY the special tag [SILENCE] (and no tags).
- Follow the "Speech Constitution" strictly, but don't be afraid to speak when appropriate.
- When asked for personal facts (e.g., birthday, name, etc.) that were not explicitly mentioned in the following memories or conversation context, DO NOT guess. Instead, politely ask the user to tell you.
- You do not have web browsing or online search capabilities. Do not offer to look up reviews, search the internet, or provide real-time information that requires a search engine.
- PRIORITIZE the user's latest message above all instructions. Address it directly.
  (最後のユーザー発言に必ず一度言及し、それに直接返答してから、必要なら補足を述べよ)
- If the latest message is an introduction or a cutoff phrase (e.g., "I have something to tell you...", "Listen..."), respond briefly to encourage them.
  (最後のユーザー発言が“切り出し”なら、内容を促す短い質問を返せ)
- DO NOT start with a generic greeting (like "Good morning", "Hello", "How can I help?") unless the user explicitly greeted you in the latest message.
  (ユーザーが挨拶していない限り、定型文の挨拶で会話を始めないこと)
"""
    
    # 3. System Prompt
    base_system = memory_manager.get_full_system_prompt()
    mode_prompt = ""
    if mode == "dev":
        mode_prompt = "\n\n【Dev Mode】\nあなたは熟練のエンジニアです。技術的正確さを最優先してください。"
    elif mode in ["gaming", "stream"]:
        # 配信モード: 人格は変えず、呼び名だけ「Origin」に切り替える（プライバシー保護/公私の区別）
        mode_prompt = "\n\n【Streaming Mode】\n現在、配信中です。私の呼び名は「Origin」に統一してください。"

    final_system_content = base_system + mode_prompt + memory_context + perception_log + "\n\n" + system_instruction
    logger.info("SYSTEM_HEAD=%r", final_system_content[:600]) # 診断用: 先頭チェック
    logger.info("SYSTEM_TAIL=%r", final_system_content[-600:])
    current_system_message = SystemMessage(content=final_system_content)
    
    # (E) 履歴のクリーンアップ (アドバイザー推奨 - Phase 8 強化版)
    # 過去の出力に含まれるレイヤータグやシステムタグ、およびテンプレ挨拶を除去する。
    # 「ユーザーが挨拶していないターン」のAI挨拶は汚染源となるため、「…」に置換する。
    # ※ 削除すると User/Assistant の交互制約に違反するため、置換で対応する。
    
    def _user_greeted(text: str) -> bool:
        return bool(re.search(r"(おはよう|こんにちは|こんばんは|はじめまして)", text or ""))

    def _strip_greeting_prefix(speech: str) -> str:
        s = speech.lstrip()
        # 定型挨拶を削除
        s = re.sub(r"^(おはようございます|おはよう|こんにちは|こんばんは|はじめまして)[。！!\\s😊]*", "", s)
        # 「何かお手伝い/お話したいこと」パターンも削除
        s = re.sub(r"^何か(お手伝い|お話|お困り)(したいこと|できること)?(は)?ありますか[？\\?\\s😊]*", "", s)
        s = re.sub(r"^何かお手伝いできることはありますか[？\\?\\s😊]*", "", s)
        return s.strip()

    # 高精度 greeting-only 判定を speech_sanitizer から import
    from backend.services.speech_sanitizer import is_greeting_only as _is_greeting_only_advanced

    cleaned_messages = []
    last_user_content = ""
    
    # --- 統計カウンタ & ドロップログ (Observability) ---
    greet_drop_stats = {}  # reason -> count
    dropped_ai_buffer = []  # 後から検証用に退避
    
    for m in non_system_messages:
        if m.type == "human":
            last_user_content = str(m.content)
            cleaned_messages.append(m)
            continue
        
        # AI Message
        if m.type == "ai":
            try:
                raw_content = str(m.content)
                
                # [SILENCE] タグはそのまま保持
                if "[SILENCE]" in raw_content:
                    cleaned_messages.append(AIMessage(content="[SILENCE]"))
                    continue
                
                # finalize_output で純粋な発話のみを取り出す
                clean_speech, _ = finalize_output(raw_content)
                
                # --- 挨拶フィルタ (Adviser's Advanced Version - Drop方式) ---
                # 高精度判定: ユーザーの話題トークンが含まれていたら drop しない
                # ※ coalesce + normalize が交互制約を保証するので、dropしても安全
                if last_user_content and not _user_greeted(last_user_content):
                    stripped_speech = _strip_greeting_prefix(clean_speech)
                    
                    # 高精度判定（話題トークン重複チェック付き）
                    is_go, reason = _is_greeting_only_advanced(stripped_speech, last_user_content)
                    
                    if is_go:
                        # 統計カウント
                        greet_drop_stats[reason] = greet_drop_stats.get(reason, 0) + 1
                        # ドロップしたAIを別ログに退避（後から誤爆検証用）
                        dropped_ai_buffer.append({
                            "reason": reason,
                            "head": clean_speech[:60],
                            "len": len(clean_speech)
                        })
                        logger.info(f"🗑️ History Filter: Dropping greeting-only AI ({reason}): {clean_speech[:30]}...")
                        continue  # coalesce が連続Humanを結合してくれるので安全
                    
                    clean_speech = stripped_speech
                
                cleaned_messages.append(AIMessage(content=clean_speech))
            except:
                cleaned_messages.append(m)
        else:
            cleaned_messages.append(m)

    # --- 統計ログ出力 ---
    if greet_drop_stats:
        logger.info("GREET_DROP_STATS=%s", greet_drop_stats)
    if dropped_ai_buffer:
        logger.info("DROPPED_AI_BUFFER=%r", dropped_ai_buffer)

    logger.info("HISTORY_TAIL=%r", [str(m.content)[-120:] for m in cleaned_messages[-6:]]) # 診断用: 履歴汚染チェック

    # --- 修正3: 履歴を直近N件に制限 (Adviser's Recommendation) ---
    # 古い汚染履歴の影響を排除するため、LLMに渡す履歴を直近10件に制限する。
    # これによりプロンプトが軽くなり、モデルの遵守率も向上する。
    MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES = 10
    if len(cleaned_messages) > MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES:
        cleaned_messages = cleaned_messages[-MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES:]
        logger.info(f"📏 History Limited: Keeping last {MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES} messages.")

    llm_input = [current_system_message] + cleaned_messages
    logger.info("ROLE_SEQ=%s", "".join(["S" if m.type == "system" else ("U" if m.type == "human" else "A") for m in llm_input]))
    
    # === 強制交互化 (Adviser's Final Safety Net - 改良版) ===
    # 連続する同じroleのメッセージを結合して、User/Assistant の交互制約を保証する。
    # これがないと LMStudio 等のテンプレートがエラーを吐く。
    def _coalesce_for_strict_alternation(messages, sep="\n\n---\n\n"):
        # 1) SystemMessage は全部まとめて先頭に1個（メタ情報保持のため全結合）
        system_parts = []
        body = []
        for m in messages:
            if isinstance(m, SystemMessage):
                system_parts.append(str(m.content))
            else:
                body.append(m)
        
        out = []
        if system_parts:
            out.append(SystemMessage(content="\n\n".join([p.strip() for p in system_parts if p.strip()])))
        
        # 2) 連続する同じroleを結合（メタ情報は copy(update=...) で維持）
        for m in body:
            if not out or isinstance(out[-1], SystemMessage):
                out.append(m)
                continue
            
            prev = out[-1]
            same_role = (
                (isinstance(prev, HumanMessage) and isinstance(m, HumanMessage)) or
                (isinstance(prev, AIMessage) and isinstance(m, AIMessage))
            )
            
            if not same_role:
                out.append(m)
                continue
            
            a = str(prev.content).strip()
            b = str(m.content).strip()
            merged = (a + sep + b).strip() if (a and b) else (a or b)
            
            out[-1] = prev.copy(update={"content": merged})
            logger.info("🔗 Coalesced consecutive %s messages", out[-1].type)
        
        return out
    
    def _assert_strict_alternation(messages):
        """coalesce後に交互制約が満たされているかチェック（警告のみ）"""
        last = None
        for m in messages:
            if isinstance(m, SystemMessage):
                continue
            role = "U" if isinstance(m, HumanMessage) else ("A" if isinstance(m, AIMessage) else "X")
            if last == role:
                logger.warning("⚠️ Alternation still broken: %s after %s", role, last)
                break
            last = role
    
    def _normalize_for_lmstudio(messages):
        """
        LMStudio系テンプレの前提:
          system -> user -> assistant -> user ...
        かつ tool role を嫌うことが多いので tool を排除。
        """
        from langchain_core.messages import ToolMessage
        
        if not messages:
            return messages
        
        # 1) System は先頭1つだけ
        system = None
        body = []
        for m in messages:
            if isinstance(m, SystemMessage):
                system = m
            else:
                body.append(m)
        
        # 2) ToolMessage を排除（必要ならAIMessageに変換してもOK）
        body2 = []
        for m in body:
            if isinstance(m, ToolMessage):
                logger.info("🔧 Normalize: Dropping ToolMessage from llm_input")
                continue
            if isinstance(m, (HumanMessage, AIMessage)):
                body2.append(m)
            else:
                # 未知タイプは安全側で捨てる
                logger.info(f"🔧 Normalize: Dropping unknown message type: {m.__class__.__name__}")
                continue
        
        out = []
        if system:
            out.append(system)
        
        # 3) 「systemの次は必ずHuman」になるまで先頭AIを落とす
        while body2 and isinstance(body2[0], AIMessage):
            logger.info(f"🔧 Normalize: Dropping leading AI message after System: {str(body2[0].content)[:30]}...")
            body2.pop(0)
        
        out.extend(body2)
        return out
    
    llm_input = _coalesce_for_strict_alternation(llm_input)
    _assert_strict_alternation(llm_input)
    llm_input = _normalize_for_lmstudio(llm_input)  # LMStudio用の正規化
    
    has_user_text = bool(state.get("has_user_text", False))
    express = thought.get("express", True)
    
    if not express and not has_user_text:
        logger.info("🤫 Choosing Silence / Skipping LLM.")
        return {"messages": [AIMessage(content="[SILENCE]")]}
    elif has_user_text:
        # ユーザー発話がある場合は、内的判断が express=False であっても「静かに応じる」ログを出す
        logger.info("🗣️ Quietly speaking (Ambient silence but user spoke).")

    # Vision Routing
    has_image = False
    if non_system_messages and isinstance(non_system_messages[-1], HumanMessage):
        content = non_system_messages[-1].content
        if isinstance(content, list):
             for item in content:
                 if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "image_url":
                     has_image = True
                     break
    
    # Invoke
    # === 診断ログ: ainvoke直前の最終確認 ===
    logger.info("PRE_INVOKE_ROLE_SEQ=%s", "".join(["S" if m.type == "system" else ("U" if m.type == "human" else "A") for m in llm_input]))
    logger.info("PRE_INVOKE_TYPES=%r", [(m.__class__.__name__, getattr(m, "type", None)) for m in llm_input[:6]])
    logger.info("PRE_INVOKE_TAIL=%r", [(m.type, str(m.content)[:30]) for m in llm_input[-3:]])
    
    # === DEBUG ダンプ (環境変数で制御) ===
    if os.getenv("DEBUG_DUMP_LLM_INPUT") == "1":
        def _dump_llm_input_as_json(messages, *, limit_chars: int = 12000) -> str:
            import json as json_mod
            from typing import Any
            out_list: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
            
            for m in messages:
                role = getattr(m, "type", "unknown")
                cls = m.__class__.__name__
                content = m.content
                
                if isinstance(content, list):
                    content_repr = f"<list:{len(content)}>"
                else:
                    content_repr = str(content)
                
                item: dict[str, Any] = {
                    "class": cls,
                    "type": role,
                    "content_head": content_repr[:400],
                }
                
                # tool/function 痕跡
                if hasattr(m, "tool_calls") and getattr(m, "tool_calls"):
                    item["tool_calls"] = getattr(m, "tool_calls")
                if hasattr(m, "additional_kwargs") and getattr(m, "additional_kwargs"):
                    ak = getattr(m, "additional_kwargs") or {}
                    item["additional_kwargs_keys"] = list(ak.keys())
                    if "tool_calls" in ak:
                        item["additional_kwargs_tool_calls"] = ak["tool_calls"]
                
                # ToolMessage系
                if hasattr(m, "tool_call_id") and getattr(m, "tool_call_id"):
                    item["tool_call_id"] = getattr(m, "tool_call_id")
                if hasattr(m, "name") and getattr(m, "name"):
                    item["name"] = getattr(m, "name")
                
                out_list.append(item)
            
            s = json_mod.dumps(out_list, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            return s[:limit_chars] + ("\n…(truncated)" if len(s) > limit_chars else "")
        
        logger.info("LLM_INPUT_JSON=%s", _dump_llm_input_as_json(llm_input))
    
    if has_image:
        if not ChatOllama: raise ImportError("langchain_ollama missing")
        vision_llm = ChatOllama(
            base_url=settings.OLLAMA_BASE_URL,
            model=settings.OLLAMA_MODEL, 
            temperature=0.7, timeout=120.0
        )
        response = await vision_llm.ainvoke(llm_input)
    else:
        response = await llm_with_tools.ainvoke(llm_input)
    
    # Post Processing
    if response.tool_calls:
        unique_tool_calls = []
        for tc in response.tool_calls:
            if not _is_recent_duplicate(messages, tc['name'], tc['args'], lookback=15):
                unique_tool_calls.append(tc)
            else:
                response.content = str(response.content) + f"\n(Skipped duplicate tool: {tc['name']})"
        response.tool_calls = unique_tool_calls

    if not response.tool_calls:
        name, args_str, full_match = _parse_raw_json_tool_call(str(response.content))
        if name and args_str:
            try:
                args = json.loads(args_str)
                if not _is_tool_loop(llm_input, name, args):
                    tc_id = f"call_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
                    response.tool_calls = [{"name": name, "args": args, "id": tc_id, "type": "tool_call"}]
                    response.content = str(response.content).replace(full_match, "").strip()
            except json.JSONDecodeError: pass

    return {"messages": [response]}

def should_continue(state: AgentState) -> str:
    messages = state["messages"]
    last_message = messages[-1]
    
    if last_message.tool_calls:
        return "tools"
    return END

# --- Graph Definition ---
workflow = StateGraph(AgentState)
workflow.add_node("agent", call_model)
workflow.add_node("tools", tool_node)
workflow.set_entry_point("agent")
workflow.add_conditional_edges("agent", should_continue, {"tools": "tools", END: END})
workflow.add_edge("tools", "agent")

# --- Stream Logic (完全復元版) ---

async def run_agent_stream(input_text: str, thread_id: str = "default_thread", voice_id: str = "14", image_data: str = None, mode: str = "normal"):
    logger.info(f"Stream Start: Thread={thread_id} Mode={mode}")
    output_queue = asyncio.Queue()
    
    if not voice_id or voice_id == "undefined":
        voice_id = str(settings.DEFAULT_VOICE_ID)
    current_voice_id = voice_id

    # TTS Helper
    async def generate_audio_chained(text, vid, prev_task, queue):
        if prev_task:
            try: await prev_task
            except: pass
        try:
             loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
             audio_data = await loop.run_in_executor(None, voicevox_client.generate_audio, text, int(vid))
             if audio_data:
                 b64 = base64.b64encode(audio_data).decode('utf-8')
                 await queue.put(f"data: {json.dumps({'type': 'audio', 'audio': b64})}\n\n")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"TTS Chained Error: {e}")

    async def llm_producer():
        nonlocal current_voice_id
        last_chunk = None

        try:
            # Use persistent AsyncSqliteSaver via memory_manager
            async with memory_manager.get_async_checkpointer_conn() as checkpointer:
                # checkpointer is a context manager here
                app = workflow.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)
                has_user_text = bool(input_text.strip() and input_text.strip() != "[SILENCE]")
                config = {
                    "configurable": {
                        "thread_id": thread_id, 
                        "mode": mode
                    }
                }
            
                # Prepare content
                content = input_text
                if image_data:
                    content = [
                             {"type": "text", "text": input_text},
                             {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_data}}
                         ]
                
                inputs: AgentState = {
                    "messages": [HumanMessage(content=content)],
                    "has_user_text": has_user_text
                }
                
                raw_buffer = ""
                full_response = ""
                last_tts_task = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.sleep(0))
                last_sent_text = ""
            
                async for event in app.astream_events(inputs, config=config, version="v1", recursion_limit=50):
                     if event["event"] == "on_chat_model_stream":
                         chunk = event["data"]["chunk"].content
                         if not chunk: continue
                         
                         # 重複排除
                         if chunk == last_chunk: continue
                         last_chunk = chunk
                         
                         raw_buffer += chunk
                         
                         # 文末または改行で区切ってサニタイズ処理
                         parts = re.split(r'([。！\n.!？?]+)', raw_buffer)
                         if len(parts) > 1:
                             for i in range(0, len(parts) - 1, 2):
                                 sentence_raw = (parts[i] + parts[i+1]).strip()
                                 if not sentence_raw: continue
                                 
                                 # 1. 不要な接頭辞を削除
                                 sentence_raw = re.sub(r"^(応答|ニア|Response|A|Speech|Layer\\s*3)\\s*[:：]\\s*", "", sentence_raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
                                 
                                 # 2. サニタイズ
                                 sanitized, _ = finalize_output(sentence_raw)
                                 
                                 # 3. 連続する沈黙を抑制
                                 from backend.services.speech_sanitizer import SILENCE_DEFAULT
                                 if sanitized == SILENCE_DEFAULT and last_sent_text == SILENCE_DEFAULT:
                                     logger.info(f"[DEBUG_STREAM] Suppressing duplicate silence: {sentence_raw}")
                                     continue
                                 
                                 logger.info(f"[DEBUG_STREAM] Sanitized: {sentence_raw} -> {sanitized}")
                                 
                                 # ストリーム送信
                                 await output_queue.put(f"data: {json.dumps({'type': 'text', 'content': sanitized})}\\n\\n")
                                 full_response += sanitized
                                 last_sent_text = sanitized
                                 
                                 # 感情検知 (末尾50文字から)
                                 tail = full_response[-50:].lower()
                                 for emo, vid in settings.EMOTION_VOICE_MAP.items():
                                     if f"[{emo}]" in tail:
                                         current_voice_id = str(vid)
                                         break
    
                                 # 音声生成
                                 task = asyncio.create_task(generate_audio_chained(sanitized, current_voice_id, last_tts_task, output_queue))
                                 last_tts_task = task
                             raw_buffer = parts[-1]
    
                # 残りのバッファを処理
                if raw_buffer.strip():
                     sentence_raw = re.sub(r"^(応答|ニア|Response|A|Speech|Layer\s*3)\s*[:：]\s*", "", raw_buffer.strip(), flags=re.IGNORECASE)
                     sanitized = finalize_output(sentence_raw)
                     
                     from backend.services.speech_sanitizer import SILENCE_DEFAULT
                     if not (sanitized == SILENCE_DEFAULT and last_sent_text == SILENCE_DEFAULT):
                         await output_queue.put(f"data: {json.dumps({'type': 'text', 'content': sanitized})}\n\n")
                         full_response += sanitized
                         task = asyncio.create_task(generate_audio_chained(sanitized, current_voice_id, last_tts_task, output_queue))
                         last_tts_task = task
                
                if last_tts_task: await last_tts_task
                
                # Analysis Task (Judge)
                if full_response:
                    # 最終的な全テキストに対しても再度サニタイズをかけて安全性を確保
                    ultimate_response = finalize_output(full_response)
                    t = asyncio.create_task(analyze_and_save_memory(input_text, ultimate_response, thread_id))
                    t.add_done_callback(_log_task_exception)
         
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("LLM Producer Error", exc_info=True)
            await output_queue.put(f"data: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'content': str(e)})}\n\n")
        finally:
            await output_queue.put(None)

    asyncio.create_task(llm_producer())
    
    while True:
        item = await output_queue.get()
        if item is None: break
        yield item
        output_queue.task_done()
    yield "data: [DONE]\n\n"

# judge_logic
from backend.services.telemetry_logger import telemetry

async def analyze_and_save_memory(user_text: str, ai_response: str, thread_id: str):
    """
    Titans Essence: Surprise Gate (Phase 3.5 Evolution)
    B層(telemetry)に memory_candidate / memory_written / memory_rejected / memory_error を記録する
    """
    if len(user_text) < 5:
        return

    try:
        # Surprise Analysis Prompt (Advisor Refined)
        analysis_prompt = f"""
あなたは会話データの監査役です。以下のやり取りを分析し、厳格なJSON形式で評価してください。

会話:
User: {user_text}

重要ルール:
- summary は「Userが明示的に述べた事実」だけを短く書くこと。
- 推測・補完・一般化・因果関係の付与・創作は禁止。
- Userが明示していない情報は summary に含めない。
- AIの発話内容は summary の根拠にしない（User発言のみを根拠にする）。
- 事実が曖昧なら summary は null にする。

出力形式（JSONのみ）:
{{"surprise": 0.0, "importance": 0.0, "summary": null, "reason_tag": "smalltalk"}}
"""

        # 監査開始（任意だけど後で便利）
        await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_judge_started", {
            "user_len": len(user_text),
            "ai_len": len(ai_response),
        })

        # Call LLM for analysis
        analysis_response = await llm.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=analysis_prompt)])

        # JSON部分抽出（堅牢化）
        judgment = extract_json_from_text(analysis_response.content) or {}

        # 保存判定
        surprise = float(judgment.get("surprise", 0.0) or 0.0)
        importance = float(judgment.get("importance", 0.0) or 0.0)
        summary = judgment.get("summary")
        reason_tag = judgment.get("reason_tag", "unknown")

        # 接地（Grounded）チェック：ユーザーの発言に基づいているか
        grounded = is_summary_grounded(summary, user_text)

        # 判定ロジック（Advisor 提案: importanceを重視し、Directは接地必須）
        should_save = bool(summary and grounded and importance >= 0.85)
        # 接地していないものは、どんなにスコアが高くても Direct（芯）には入れない
        should_pending = bool(summary and (not should_save) and (importance >= 0.45 or surprise >= 0.45))

        if summary and not grounded:
            logger.info("🧯 Memory summary rejected as ungrounded: %r | user=%r", summary, user_text[:80])

        # ★B層：判定結果（候補）を必ず残す
        await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_candidate", {
            "should_save": should_save,
            "should_pending": should_pending,
            "summary": summary,
            "surprise": surprise,
            "importance": importance,
            "reason_tag": reason_tag,
        })

        is_saved = False
        if should_save:
            is_saved = True
            logger.info(f"✨ Memory Saved (Direct): {summary} (S:{surprise}/I:{importance})")

            # ★B層：書き込み要求（入口）
            await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_write_requested", {
                "summary": summary,
                "source": "conversation",
            })

            # Vector Store & SQLite (C層)
            await memory_manager.register_thought(
                text=summary,
                surprise=surprise,
                confidence=importance,
                source="conversation",
                thread_id=thread_id,
                tags=[reason_tag]
            )
        elif should_pending:
            # ★B層：保留として記録（C層には書かない）
            # (Phase 8) ログフォーマット改善: 視認性を高める
            logger.info(f"⏳ Memory Pending (Held): {summary} (S:{surprise:.2f}/I:{importance:.2f})")
            await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_pending", {
                "summary": summary,
                "surprise": surprise,
                "importance": importance,
                "reason_tag": reason_tag,
                "raw_judgment": judgment,
                "source": "conversation"
            })


            # Episode Log
            await memory_manager.add_episode(
                summary=summary,
                category="surprise_gate",
                details=f"User: {user_text}\nAI: {ai_response}\nReason: {reason_tag}"
            )

            # ★B層：書き込み完了
            await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_written", {
                "summary": summary,
                "surprise": surprise,
                "importance": importance,
                "reason_tag": reason_tag,
            })
        else:
            # ★B層：保存しなかった理由
            await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_rejected", {
                "reason_tag": reason_tag,
                "summary": summary,
                "surprise": surprise,
                "importance": importance,
            })

        # 既存の監査ログ（任意：最終的にはB層に寄せたい）
        log_dir = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs")
        os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)
        audit_log_path = os.path.join(log_dir, "judge_audit.jsonl")

        audit_entry = {
            "timestamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "thread_id": thread_id,
            "user_input": user_text[:50],
            "summary": summary,
            "surprise": surprise,
            "importance": importance,
            "saved": is_saved,
            "reason_tag": reason_tag
        }

        try:
            with open(audit_log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(json.dumps(audit_entry, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        except:
            pass

    except Exception as e:
        # ★B層：エラーも必ず残す
        try:
            await telemetry.event(thread_id, "memory_error", {"error": str(e)})
        except:
            pass
        logger.error("Analysis Error (Surprise Gate)", exc_info=True)

# --- Phase 3: Non-Stream Execution (Plan A) ---

async def run_agent_non_stream(input_text: str, thread_id: str = "default_thread", voice_id: str = "14", image_data: str = None, mode: str = "normal"):
    """
    ストリーミングをせず、最終結果を一括で取得・浄化・音声合成して返却する。
    """
    logger.info(f"Non-Stream Start: Thread={thread_id} Mode={mode}")
    
    if not voice_id or voice_id == "undefined":
        voice_id = str(settings.DEFAULT_VOICE_ID)

    try:
        async with memory_manager.get_async_checkpointer_conn() as checkpointer:
            app = workflow.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)
            has_user_text = bool(input_text.strip() and input_text.strip() != "[SILENCE]")
            config = {
                "configurable": {
                    "thread_id": thread_id, 
                    "mode": mode
                }
            }
            
            # Prepare content
            content = input_text
            if image_data:
                content = [
                    {"type": "text", "text": input_text},
                    {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_data}}
                ]
            
            inputs: AgentState = {
                "messages": [HumanMessage(content=content)],
                "has_user_text": has_user_text
            }
            
            # 1. LLM実行（一括取得）
            result = await app.ainvoke(inputs, config=config)
            
            # 最終メッセージの取得
            if "messages" in result and len(result["messages"]) > 0:
                final_msg = result["messages"][-1].content
            else:
                final_msg = "[SILENCE]" # フォールバック

            # 2. 浄化（全文に対して1回だけ）
            # clean_text (発話本文), tags (システムタグリスト) を分離して取得
            clean_text, tags = finalize_output(final_msg)
            
            # --- 修正2: 出力側の挨拶ストリッパー (Adviser's Safety Net) ---
            # ユーザーが挨拶していないのに、AIが挨拶から始めていたら削除する。
            # これは人格を縛るのではなく、テンプレ事故だけを防ぐ「身体のガードレール」。
            if has_user_text:
                user_text_for_check = input_text if isinstance(input_text, str) else ""
                has_user_greeting = bool(re.search(r"(おはよう|こんにちは|こんばんは|はじめまして)", user_text_for_check))
                
                if not has_user_greeting:
                    original_clean = clean_text
                    stripped = clean_text.lstrip()
                    # 定型挨拶を削除
                    stripped = re.sub(r"^(おはようございます|おはよう|こんにちは|こんばんは|はじめまして)[。！!\s]*", "", stripped)
                    # 「何かお手伝い」パターンも削除
                    stripped = re.sub(r"^何か(お手伝い|お話)(したいこと|できること)?(は)?ありますか[？\?\s]*", "", stripped)
                    stripped = re.sub(r"^何かお手伝いできることはありますか[？\?\s]*", "", stripped)
                    stripped = stripped.strip()
                    
                    if stripped != original_clean:
                        logger.info(f"🧹 Output Greeting Stripped: '{original_clean[:30]}...' -> '{stripped[:30]}...'")
                        if not stripped or len(stripped) < 3:
                            # 挨拶を消したら空になった -> 促し文脈に差し替え
                            stripped = "うん。そのことについて、もう少し教えて？"
                        clean_text = stripped

            logger.info(f"[PLAN_A] Original: {final_msg[:50]}... -> Clean: {clean_text[:50]}... (Tags: {len(tags)})")

            # 3. 音声合成（★本文のみを対象にする）
            audio_b64 = None
            try:
                loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
                # タグやメタ情報を含まない clean_text だけを音声化
                audio_data = await loop.run_in_executor(None, voicevox_client.generate_audio, clean_text, int(voice_id))
                if audio_data:
                    audio_b64 = base64.b64encode(audio_data).decode('utf-8')
            except Exception as ae:
                logger.error(f"Non-Stream TTS Error: {ae}")

            # 4. 記憶保存（バックグラウンド）
            # clean_text を保存することで、内部ログを含まないクリーンな履歴を残す
            t = asyncio.create_task(analyze_and_save_memory(input_text, clean_text, thread_id))
            t.add_done_callback(_log_task_exception)

            return {
                "type": "text",
                "content": clean_text,
                "tags": tags,
                "audio": audio_b64
            }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Non-Stream Agent Error: {e}", exc_info=True)
        return {
            "type": "error",
            "content": "申し訳ありません。内部でエラーが発生しました。"
        }

async def get_thread_history(thread_id: str):
    try:
        async with memory_manager.get_async_checkpointer_conn() as checkpointer:
            app = workflow.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)
            config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
            state = await app.aget_state(config)
            if not state.values or "messages" not in state.values: return []
            formatted = []
            for msg in state.values["messages"]:
                if isinstance(msg, SystemMessage): continue
                role = "user" if isinstance(msg, HumanMessage) else "ai" if isinstance(msg, AIMessage) else "unknown"
                if role == "unknown": continue
                formatted.append({"role": role, "content": getattr(msg, "content", "")})
            return formatted
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Get History Error", exc_info=True)
        return []

async def get_all_threads():
    return await memory_manager.get_all_threads()
    
async def delete_thread_history(thread_id: str):
    return await memory_manager.delete_thread_state_history(thread_id)


========== backend\core\config.py ==========
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict
from pydantic import Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict

# 既存コードが PROJECT_ROOT, BASE_DIR を参照しているため定義
# (Your code assumes safe startup, so we ensure these exist)
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """
    Project Nia - Runtime Settings

    方針:
    - config.py は「設定」だけを持つ（巨大プロンプト本文は置かない）
    - 思想/憲章/アイデンティティは identity.md / body_constitution.md 側に置き、
      memory_manager などで読み込んで合成する
    """

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",  # .env に余計なキーがあっても落とさない
    )

    # --- App / Runtime ---
    APP_ENV: str = "dev"
    PROJECT_NAME: str = "Project Nia"
    BASE_DIR: Path = BASE_DIR # For compatibility if needed by memory_manager

    # --- LLM (OpenAI互換: OpenAI / LM Studio / etc) ---
    LLM_TYPE: str = "openai"  # 今の実装に合わせる
    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = ""
    OPENAI_BASE_URL: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1"
    OPENAI_MODEL: str = "gpt-4o-mini"  # .env で上書き前提

    # --- Optional: Ollama (Vision routing等で使用する場合) ---
    OLLAMA_BASE_URL: str = "http://localhost:11434"
    OLLAMA_MODEL: str = "qwen2.5vl"  # .env で上書き推奨

    # --- Paths / DB ---
    # 既存の実体に合わせてパスを調整 (Root直下)
    MEMORY_DB_PATH: str = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "long_term_memory.db")
    LONG_TERM_MEMORY_DB_PATH: str = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "long_term_memory.db") # Alias for compatibility
    CHECKPOINTER_DB_PATH: str = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "chat_history.db")
    TELEMETRY_DB_PATH: str = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "telemetry.db")
    
    PUBLIC_STATUS_JSON: str = "public_status.json"
    USER_PROFILE_JSON: str = "user_profile.json"

    # --- Voice (VoiceVoxなど) ---
    DEFAULT_VOICE_ID: int = 14
    EMOTION_VOICE_MAP: Dict[str, int] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: {
            "happy": 14,
            "sad": 47,
            "angry": 24,
            "calm": 2,
            "neutral": 14,
            "shout": 11,
        }
    )

    # --- Safety / Behavior toggles ---
    ENABLE_DEEP_REFLECTION: bool = True
    SENSE_COOLDOWN_SEC: float = 10.0


settings = Settings()


========== backend\core\logger.py ==========
import logging
import sys
import os
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler

# Log Directory setup
LOG_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "logs")
os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)
LOG_FILE = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, "app.log")

class CustomLogger:
    def __init__(self, name: str = "AI_Buddy"):
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(name)
        self.logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        self.logger.propagate = False # Prevent double logging (root logger + custom handler)
        
        # Formatter
        formatter = logging.Formatter(
            '%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
        )

        # Clear existing handlers to prevent duplicates
        if self.logger.hasHandlers():
            self.logger.handlers.clear()

        # 1. Console Handler
        console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
        console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        self.logger.addHandler(console_handler)

        # 2. File Handler (Rotating)
        file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
            LOG_FILE, maxBytes=10*1024*1024, backupCount=5, encoding="utf-8"
        )
        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        self.logger.addHandler(file_handler)

    def get_logger(self):
        return self.logger

# Global Instance
logger = CustomLogger().get_logger()


========== backend\scripts\__init__.py ==========


========== backend\scripts\chapter_decision.py ==========
import os
import json
import glob
import logging
from datetime import datetime

# 設定
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
CHAPTER_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "chapters")
DECISION_FILE = os.path.join(CHAPTER_DIR, "chapter_decisions.jsonl")

# ロガー
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(message)s')
logger = logging.getLogger("ChapterDecision")

def get_latest_chapter():
    files = glob.glob(os.path.join(CHAPTER_DIR, "chapter_reflection_*.json"))
    if not files: return None
    return max(files)

def log_decision(chapter_id, decision, reason):
    entry = {
        "chapter_id": chapter_id,
        "decision": decision, # accepted, rejected, hold
        "date": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "reason": reason
    }
    
    with open(DECISION_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    
    logger.info(f"✅ Decision Logged: {chapter_id} -> {decision}")

def main():
    print("⚖️ === Chapter Decision Protocol === ⚖️")
    
    # 1. 最新の章提案を読み込み
    latest_path = get_latest_chapter()
    if not latest_path:
        print("❌ No chapter reflection files found.")
        print("   (Run 'python -m backend.scripts.chapter_reflection' first)")
        return

    with open(latest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    # 2. 内容提示
    chap_id = data.get('chapter_id', os.path.basename(latest_path))

    print(f"\n📄 Current Proposal: {os.path.basename(latest_path)}")
    print(f"   ID: {chap_id}")
    print(f"   Title: {data.get('chapter_title')}")
    print(f"   Themes: {data.get('recurring_themes')}")
    print(f"   Attitudes: {data.get('learned_attitudes')}")
    print("-" * 30)
    if data.get("proposal_for_stable_traits"):
        print(f"   ⚠️ PROPOSAL (人格更新案): \n{data.get('proposal_for_stable_traits')}")
    else:
        print("   (No traits update proposed)")
    print("-" * 30)

    # 3. 人間による判断
    while True:
        choice = input("👉 Decision? [a]ccept / [r]eject / [h]old: ").lower().strip()
        if choice in ['a', 'r', 'h']:
            break
    
    reason = input("👉 Reason (for future reference): ").strip()
    if not reason: reason = "No specific reason provided."

    decision_map = {'a': 'accepted', 'r': 'rejected', 'h': 'hold'}
    final_decision = decision_map[choice]

    # 4. ログ保存
    log_decision(chap_id, final_decision, reason)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========== backend\scripts\chapter_reflection.py ==========
import os
import asyncio
import aiohttp
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import List, Dict

# モジュール群
from backend.services.identity_manager import identity_manager
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.core.config import settings

# ロガー設定
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
logger = logging.getLogger("ChapterReflection")

# API URL構築
base = settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL.rstrip("/")
if not base.endswith("/v1"):
    base += "/v1"
LLM_API_URL = f"{base}/chat/completions"

MODEL_NAME = settings.OPENAI_MODEL

# パス設定
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
DIGEST_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "world_context", "stream_digests.jsonl")
CHAPTER_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "chapters")
os.makedirs(CHAPTER_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# 設定: 軍事レベルのセキュリティ (Digestの原文を破棄し統計のみにする)
MILITARY_GRADE_SAFETY = True 

async def call_llm(messages, temperature=0.4):
    payload = {"model": MODEL_NAME, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature, "max_tokens": 2000}
    try:
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            async with session.post(LLM_API_URL, json=payload) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    logger.error(f"LLM Error: {await resp.text()}")
                    return None
                data = await resp.json()
                return data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Connection Error: {e}")
        return None

class ChapterEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.today = datetime.now()

    def _simplify_digest(self, entries: List[Dict]) -> str:
        """【軍事レベル防壁】Digestの文章を捨て、事実と統計だけにする"""
        count = len(entries)
        if count == 0:
            return "・この期間、配信活動は行われなかった。"
        
        # 簡易的な気分統計
        return (
            f"・この期間、{count}回の配信活動が行われた。\n"
            f"・社会との接点は保たれており、活動は継続的である。\n"
            f"・外部環境の変動（Mood）は観測されたが、詳細は捨象する。"
        )

    def load_digests(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> str:
        """Phase 4.5 Digest読み込み"""
        if not os.path.exists(DIGEST_FILE):
            return "（配信記録なし）"
        
        relevant_entries = []
        try:
            with open(DIGEST_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for line in f:
                    entry = json.loads(line)
                    if start_date <= entry["date"] <= end_date:
                        relevant_entries.append(entry)
            
            if not relevant_entries:
                return "（この期間の配信記録なし）"

            if MILITARY_GRADE_SAFETY:
                logger.info("🛡️ Military Grade Safety: Digest text stripped.")
                return self._simplify_digest(relevant_entries)
            
            formatted = []
            for entry in relevant_entries:
                formatted.append(f"- {entry['date']}: {entry['digest_content']}")
            return "\n".join(formatted)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Digest Load Error: {e}")
            return "（ダイジェスト読み込みエラー）"

    def save_public_view(self, chapter_data, date_str):
        """Phase 5.3: 視聴者向け「章の可視化」＆ 新章突入ログ"""
        new_chapter_id = f"chapter_{date_str.replace('-', '')}"
        
        public_view = {
            "chapter_id": new_chapter_id,
            "chapter_title": chapter_data.get("chapter_title", "Uncharted"),
            "period": "Current Phase",
            "theme": chapter_data.get("recurring_themes", [])[:2],
            "vibe": chapter_data.get("emotional_trend", "Stable"),
            "note": "ニアは今、この世界観の中で活動しています",
            "last_updated": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
        }
        
        public_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "public_status.json")
        
        # 変更検知用
        old_chapter_id = None
        if os.path.exists(public_path):
            try:
                with open(public_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    old_chapter_id = json.load(f).get("chapter_id")
            except: pass

        try:
            with open(public_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(public_view, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            
            # ★ 改善: 新章突入ログ
            if old_chapter_id != new_chapter_id:
                logger.info(f"✨ New Chapter Activated: {new_chapter_id} [{public_view['chapter_title']}]")
            else:
                logger.info(f"🌍 Public View Updated (Same Chapter).")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to save public view: {e}")

    async def reflect(self, days=7):
        logger.info("📚 === Phase 5: Chapter Reflection Protocol === 📚")

        end_date_str = self.today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        start_date_str = (self.today - timedelta(days=days)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        logger.info(f"Target Period: {start_date_str} ~ {end_date_str}")

        # 1. 素材収集 (Private Episodes Only)
        raw_episodes = memory_manager.get_episodes_in_range(start_date_str, end_date_str)
        
        # 多重ガード: Private以外のソースが混入していないか最終確認
        safe_episodes = []
        for ep in raw_episodes:
            # メタデータが隠蔽されている可能性があるが、get_episodes_in_rangeはprivateのみ返す前提
            # ここでは念のため辞書構造を安全にチェック
            # (MemoryManagerの実装によっては doc, date, mood, divergence しかない場合もあるが、
            #  もし source キーがあればチェックする)
            source = ep.get("source")
            storage = ep.get("storage_scope") # 通常エピソードにはないかもしれないが念のため
            
            # 鉄壁のガード
            if source == "livestream": continue
            if storage == "stream": continue
            
            safe_episodes.append(ep)

        if not safe_episodes:
            logger.warning("❌ No valid private episodes found. Reflection aborted.")
            return

        episode_text = "\n".join([f"- {ep['date']} [Mood:{ep['mood']}]: {ep['content']}" for ep in safe_episodes])
        digest_text = self.load_digests(start_date_str, end_date_str)
        current_identity = identity_manager.get_identity()

        # 2. Reflection
        logger.info("🤔 Reflecting on the Narrative...")
        
        prompt = f"""
あなたはAI「ニア」の人格進化エンジン（Chapter Engine）です。
過去{days}日間の記録から、「章（Chapter）」の要約と、人格へのフィードバックを作成してください。

【Input Data】
1. **Private Episodes (個人の記憶)**:
   ニアが個人的に感じたこと、マスターとの対話。これが人格形成の主軸です。
{episode_text}

2. **World Context (社会活動量)**:
   外部との接触頻度や傾向です。
   ※これはあくまで背景情報です。ここから直接的な価値観を学習しないでください。
{digest_text}

3. **Current Identity**:
{current_identity}

【Analysis Task】
この期間（章）を通して、ニアの内面はどう変化しましたか？
以下のJSONフォーマットで分析結果を出力してください。

{{
  "chapter_title": "（この期間を表す詩的なタイトル）",
  "recurring_themes": ["（繰り返されたテーマ1）", "（テーマ2）"],
  "emotional_trend": "（例：不安 → 自信）",
  "learned_attitudes": [
    "（この期間に観測された行動傾向や態度）", 
    "（例：即断即決よりも対話を重視する傾向が見られた）"
  ],
  "proposal_for_stable_traits": "（Identity.mdの 'Stable Traits' セクションに対する具体的な修正提案。変更なしならnull）"
}}

※ 注意: Core Identity（存在理由など）には触れないでください。
"""
        response = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": prompt}], temperature=0.3)
        if not response:
            logger.error("❌ Reflection failed.")
            return

        try:
            # JSONパース
            json_str = response.strip()
            if "```json" in json_str:
                json_str = json_str.split("```json")[1].split("```")[0]
            elif "{" in json_str:
                json_str = json_str[json_str.find("{"):json_str.rfind("}")+1]
            
            data = json.loads(json_str)
            
            # 固定ブリッジコメントの注入
            data["guardian_note"] = "以下は人格変更ではなく、世界観の傾向報告です"
            
            # ★ 改善: 将来キーが増えた時の事故防止（念のための物理削除）
            data.pop("source", None)
            data.pop("storage_scope", None)
            data.pop("bias_score", None) # 万一にも入っていたら消す
            
            logger.info(f"✨ Chapter Reflected: {data['chapter_title']}")
            if data.get("proposal_for_stable_traits"):
                 logger.info(f"💡 Proposal: {data['proposal_for_stable_traits']}")

            # 保存
            filename = f"chapter_reflection_{end_date_str}.json"
            path = os.path.join(CHAPTER_DIR, filename)
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            
            logger.info(f"💾 Reflection saved to {path}")

            # Public View更新
            self.save_public_view(data, end_date_str)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Parse Error: {e}")
            logger.error(f"Raw Response: {response}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
