    import sys
    if sys.platform == 'win32': asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
    
    engine = ChapterEngine()
    asyncio.run(engine.reflect(days=7))


========== backend\scripts\dump_all.py ==========
import os

PROJECT_ROOT = r"C:\Users\jk023\Desktop\AI_Buddy"
BASE_OUTPUT_NAME = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "project_dump")
MAX_FILE_SIZE = 2 * 1024 * 1024  # 2MB per file

# 除外リスト (ノイズになるもの)
IGNORE_DIRS = {
    ".git", ".venv", "node_modules", ".next", "__pycache__", 
    "logs", "chroma_db", "obs_output", "sleep_outputs", "docs", "tmp", "venv", ".idea", ".vscode",
    "saved_memories_critical", "faiss_index", "identity_snapshots", "backups"
}
IGNORE_FILES = {
    "project_dump.txt", "package-lock.json", "yarn.lock", "pnpm-lock.yaml", 
    "dump_all.py", ".DS_Store", "README.txt", "chat_history.db"
}
IGNORE_EXTS = {
    ".pyc", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".ico", ".jsonl", ".sqlite3", 
    ".pkl", ".bin", ".exe", ".dll", ".so", ".dylib", ".mp3", ".wav",
    ".db", ".zip", ".log", ".gz", ".tar"
}

class RollingFileWriter:
    def __init__(self, base_name, max_size):
        self.base_name = base_name
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.part = 1
        self.current_file = None
        self.current_filename = None
        self._open_next()

    def _open_next(self):
        if self.current_file:
            self.current_file.close()
        
        self.current_filename = f"{self.base_name}_{self.part:02d}.txt"
        self.current_file = open(self.current_filename, "w", encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"--> Creating new dump file: {self.current_filename}")
        
        header = f"Project Dump Part {self.part} generated at {os.environ.get('TIME', 'Unknown')}\n\n"
        self.current_file.write(header)
        self.part += 1

    def write(self, data):
        # 概算サイズチェック（厳密でなくて良い）
        if self.current_file.tell() + len(data) > self.max_size:
            self._open_next()
        self.current_file.write(data)

    def close(self):
        if self.current_file:
            self.current_file.close()

def dump_files():
    print(f"Starting dump from: {PROJECT_ROOT}")
    writer = RollingFileWriter(BASE_OUTPUT_NAME, MAX_FILE_SIZE)

    try:
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_ROOT):
            # 除外ディレクトリを探索対象から外す
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for f in files:
                if f in IGNORE_FILES: continue
                if f.startswith("."): continue 
                
                ext = os.path.splitext(f)[1].lower()
                if ext in IGNORE_EXTS: continue
                
                # 分割ファイル自体を除外
                if f.startswith("project_dump_"): continue

                full_path = os.path.join(root, f)
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(full_path, PROJECT_ROOT)

                # Skip if file path contains ignored dirs (safety catch)
                if any(d in rel_path.split(os.sep) for d in IGNORE_DIRS):
                    continue

                try:
                    with open(full_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors='ignore') as infile:
                        content = infile.read()
                        # Remove unusual line terminators
                        content = content.replace('\u2028', '\n').replace('\u2029', '\n')
                        
                        out_chunk = ""
                        out_chunk += "=" * 50 + "\n"
                        out_chunk += f"FILE: {rel_path}\n"
                        out_chunk += "=" * 50 + "\n"
                        out_chunk += content + "\n\n"
                        
                        writer.write(out_chunk)
                        # print(f"Added: {rel_path}")
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Skipped {rel_path}: {e}")

    finally:
        writer.close()
        print("Done.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    dump_files()


========== backend\scripts\generate_docs.py ==========
import os

# プロジェクトルートの特定
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
DOCS_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "docs")

# 1. 運用マニュアル (OPERATION_MANUAL.md)
MANUAL_TEXT = """# 📘 ニア(Near) 運用マニュアル

## 📅 1. 日次ルーティン (Daily Loop)
**目的:** ニアと話し、配信し、一日の終わりに記憶を整理する。

### ① 起動 (Start)
1. **LLMサーバー起動:** LM Studio / Ollama 等を起動。
2. **広報官 (OBS連携) 起動:**
   ```bash
   python -m backend.scripts.obs_writer
   ```
3. **ニア本体 起動:**
   ```bash
   python -m backend.core.agent
   ```

### ② 活動中 (Activity)
- **会話:** 普通に話しかける。
- **配信:** OBSに `obs_output/` 内のテキストファイルを表示。
    - `chapter_title.txt`: 今の章タイトル
    - `current_vibe.txt`: 今の気分

### ③ 就寝 (Sleep Cycle)
**★重要: 一日の終わりに必ず実行。**
```bash
python -m backend.scripts.sleep_cycle
```
- **処理内容:**
    - 記憶の整理（Private Memoryへの定着）。
    - 配信ノイズ（Stream Memory）の無視・破棄。
    - 人格の微調整（Guardianによる保護）。

## 🗓️ 2. 週次ルーティン (Weekly Evolution)
**目的:** 過去1週間（1つの章）を振り返り、人格を進化させるか決める。
**推奨:** 週末に手動実行。

### ① 章の生成 (Reflection)
```bash
python -m backend.scripts.chapter_reflection
```
- **結果:** `logs/chapters/` に振り返りデータが生成される。
- **注意:** この時点では人格は変わらない。

### ② 人間による決裁 (Decision) ★最重要
```bash
python -m backend.scripts.chapter_decision
```
- 提案内容を見て判断する。
    - `[a]ccept`: 承認 (人格に反映)
    - `[r]eject`: 却下 (破棄)
    - `[h]old`: 保留 (現状維持)
- **心構え:** 「無理に変える必要はない」。Holdも立派な選択。

## 🛡️ 3. 緊急時の対応
- **変なことを言い出した:**
    - `identity.md` を開き、該当する記述（Recent Learnings等）を手動削除する。
- **章の提案がおかしい:**
    - `chapter_decision.py` で `[r]eject` する。
"""

# 2. システム仕様書 (SYSTEM_SPEC.md)
SPEC_TEXT = """# ⚙️ ニア(Near) システム仕様書

## 1. 記憶システム (The Brain)
- **Private Memory:** 信頼度100%。あなたとの会話・約束を永久保存。
- **Stream Memory:** 信頼度減衰。配信コメント等は時間が経つと自動消滅(GC)。
- **Fail-Safe:** 配信メタデータ欠損時は自動で最大警戒モードになり汚染を防ぐ。

## 2. 防御システム (The Shield)
- **Audience Safety Layer v2:** 群衆の「数」「悪意」を検知し影響力を弱める。
- **Stream Digest (Phase 4.5):** 配信記憶は物理削除され、「抽象的な雰囲気（天気図）」だけが外伝として残る。
- **Allow List:** 「学習許可」がない記憶は、寝ている間に夢に見ることすら許されない。

## 3. 人格進化 (The Soul)
- **Guardian (Layer B):** 人格の管理者。急激な変化や性格の改変をブロックする。
- **Chapter Evolution (Phase 5):** 週単位で人生を振り返り、自己分析する。
- **Human Check (Phase 5.4):** 人間が `chapter_decision` で承認しない限り、人格は絶対に書き換わらない。

## 4. インターフェース (The Interface)
- **OBS連携 (Phase 6):** `obs_writer` が人格回路と絶縁された状態でテキストを出力。

## 三権分立:
- **AI:** 考える (Reflection)
- **人間:** 決める (Decision)
- **システム:** 従う (Sleep Cycle)
"""

# 3. ディレクトリ構造 (DIRECTORY_MAP.md)
DIR_TEXT = """# 📂 ディレクトリマップ

| パス | 説明 | 重要度 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `identity.md` | ニアの魂。 ここが変わると性格が変わる。 | ★★★ |
| `backend/core/config.py` | 設定ファイル。APIキーやURLはここ。 | ★★★ |
| `logs/chapters/` | 決裁ログ。 過去の章と判断記録。 | ★★☆ |
| `logs/judge_audit.jsonl` | 会話ログ。全ての記憶判断の記録。 | ★★☆ |
| `logs/world_context/` | 外伝ダイジェスト。配信の空気感。 | ★☆☆ |
| `obs_output/` | 配信表示用テキスト。OBSはここを見る。 | ★☆☆ |
| `chroma_db/` | 記憶データベース（ベクトルストア）。 | ★★★ |
"""

def generate():
    os.makedirs(DOCS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Manual
    with open(os.path.join(DOCS_DIR, "OPERATION_MANUAL.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(MANUAL_TEXT)
    
    # Specs
    with open(os.path.join(DOCS_DIR, "SYSTEM_SPEC.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(SPEC_TEXT)
    
    # Dir Map
    with open(os.path.join(DOCS_DIR, "DIRECTORY_MAP.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(DIR_TEXT)
    
    print(f"✅ Documentation generated in: {DOCS_DIR}")
    print("   - OPERATION_MANUAL.md")
    print("   - SYSTEM_SPEC.md")
    print("   - DIRECTORY_MAP.md")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate()


========== backend\scripts\show_qr.py ==========
import socket
import os
import sys

# Windows環境での文字コードエラー対策
if sys.platform == "win32":
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')

def get_local_ip():
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        # 実際に接続はしないが、ルートを確認してIPを特定する
        s.connect(('10.255.255.255', 1))
        IP = s.getsockname()[0]
    except Exception:
        IP = '127.0.0.1'
    finally:
        s.close()
    return IP

def show_qr():
    ip = get_local_ip()
    port = "8000"
    url = f"http://{ip}:{port}"
    
    # コンソールクリア
    os.system('cls' if os.name == 'nt' else 'clear')
    
    print("\n" + "="*50)
    print(f" 📱 Scan to open Nia on Mobile")
    print(f" 🔗 {url}")
    print("="*50 + "\n")
    
    # QRコード表示試行
    try:
        import qrcode
        qr = qrcode.QRCode()
        qr.add_data(url)
        qr.print_ascii(invert=True)
    except ImportError:
        print("⚠️ 'qrcode' library not found.")
        print("   Run: pip install qrcode")
        print("   (You can still access via the URL above)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    show_qr()


========== backend\scripts\sleep_cycle.py ==========
import os
import asyncio
import aiohttp
import shutil
import json
import logging
import glob
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

# モジュール群
from backend.services.identity_manager import identity_manager
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.tools.judge_inspector import JudgeInspector
from backend.services.identity_diff import IdentityDiff
from backend.core.config import settings

# ロガー設定
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
logger = logging.getLogger("SleepCycle")

# 設定
# API URL構築
base = settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL.rstrip("/")
if base.endswith("/v1"):
    base = base[:-3]
LLM_API_URL = f"{base}/v1/chat/completions"

MODEL_NAME = settings.OPENAI_MODEL
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

# パス設定
LOG_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "judge_audit.jsonl")
ARCHIVE_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "archive")
AUTONOMOUS_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "sleep_outputs")
DIVERGENCE_LOG = os.path.join(AUTONOMOUS_DIR, "divergence_log.jsonl")
CHAPTER_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "chapters")
DECISION_FILE = os.path.join(CHAPTER_DIR, "chapter_decisions.jsonl")

os.makedirs(AUTONOMOUS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(ARCHIVE_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# --- Helper: LLM Call ---
async def call_llm(messages, temperature=0.5, max_tokens=1000):
    payload = {"model": MODEL_NAME, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature, "max_tokens": max_tokens}
    try:
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            async with session.post(LLM_API_URL, json=payload) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    logger.error(f"LLM Error: {await resp.text()}")
                    return None
                data = await resp.json()
                return data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Connection Error: {e}")
        return None

# --- Helper: Section Management ---
def extract_section_content(full_text: str, header: str) -> str:
    if header not in full_text: return ""
    after = full_text.split(header, 1)[1]
    for sep in ["\n## ", "\n# "]:
        if sep in after: return after.split(sep, 1)[0].strip()
    return after.strip()

def replace_section_content(full_text: str, header: str, new_content: str) -> str:
    if header not in full_text: return full_text.strip() + f"\n\n{header}\n{new_content}"
    pre_header, after = full_text.split(header, 1)
    next_section_start = -1
    for sep in ["\n## ", "\n# "]:
        pos = after.find(sep)
        if pos != -1:
            if next_section_start == -1 or pos < next_section_start: next_section_start = pos
    if next_section_start != -1: return f"{pre_header}{header}\n{new_content}{after[next_section_start:]}"
    else: return f"{pre_header}{header}\n{new_content}"

# --- Helper: Normalization ---
async def normalize_learnings_safe(current_text: str, new_patch: str, max_lines=6) -> str:
    combined_raw = f"【新規パッチ（最優先）】\n{new_patch}\n\n【既存の行動指針】\n{current_text}"
    editor_prompt = f"""
あなたはAI人格データの「編集者」です。以下の行動指針リストを整理してください。
【入力】
{combined_raw}
【タスク】
1. 重複する指針を統合（簡潔化）。
2. 【新規パッチ】を優先。
3. 性格変更や自己言及（AIとして〜）は削除。
4. 重要度順に最大{max_lines}行。
【出力】
- 箇条書きのみ
"""
    normalized_text = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": editor_prompt}], temperature=0.1)

    def _fallback():
        lines = [l.strip() for l in (new_patch + "\n" + current_text).splitlines() if l.strip()]
        unique = []
        for l in lines:
            if l not in unique: unique.append(l)
        return "\n".join(unique[:max_lines])

    if not normalized_text: return _fallback()
    lines = [l.strip() for l in normalized_text.splitlines() if l.strip().startswith("-")]
    
    if len(lines) > max_lines: return _fallback()
    for line in lines:
        if any(x in line for x in ["性格を変", "口調", "リセット", "初期化"]): return _fallback()

    return "\n".join(lines)

# --- Divergence Logic ---
def compute_divergence(auto_conf: float, patch_lines: int, normalized_lines: int) -> float:
    if normalized_lines == 0: return auto_conf
    acceptance_ratio = patch_lines / normalized_lines
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, abs(auto_conf - acceptance_ratio)))

def save_divergence_log(auto_conf, patch_lines, normalized_lines, mood):
    divergence = compute_divergence(auto_conf, patch_lines, normalized_lines)
    entry = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "date": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
        "auto_confidence": auto_conf,
        "guardian_patch_lines": patch_lines,
        "normalized_lines": normalized_lines,
        "divergence_score": round(divergence, 3),
        "mood": mood
    }
    with open(DIVERGENCE_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    logger.info(f"💔 Divergence: {entry['divergence_score']} (Mood: {mood})")
    return entry

# --- Phase 5: Chapter Loader & Decision Check ---
def get_chapter_decision(chapter_id):
    if not os.path.exists(DECISION_FILE):
        return None
    
    last_decision = None
    try:
        with open(DECISION_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                entry = json.loads(line)
                if entry.get("chapter_id") == chapter_id:
                    last_decision = entry.get("decision")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return last_decision

def load_latest_chapter_proposal():
    if not os.path.exists(CHAPTER_DIR):
        return None
    
    files = glob.glob(os.path.join(CHAPTER_DIR, "chapter_reflection_*.json"))
    if not files:
        return None
        
    latest_file = max(files) 
    try:
        with open(latest_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            return data
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to load chapter: {e}")
        return None

# --- Robust Filtering Helpers ---
def resolve_source(m: dict) -> str:
    """キー名揺らぎの吸収"""
    if "source" in m: return m["source"]
    meta = m.get("metadata", {})
    if isinstance(meta, dict) and "source" in meta: return meta["source"]
    scope = m.get("storage_scope") or meta.get("storage_scope")
    if scope == "stream": return "livestream"
    return "conversation"

def normalize_sleep_log(log):
    source = resolve_source(log)
    
    # allow_sleep_learning がログにない場合、sourceを見て判断する（互換性維持）
    allow_default = (source != "livestream")
    
    # メタデータ内も探す
    meta = log.get("metadata", {})
    allow_flag = log.get("allow_sleep_learning")
    if allow_flag is None and isinstance(meta, dict):
        allow_flag = meta.get("allow_sleep_learning")
        
    if allow_flag is None:
        allow_flag = allow_default
    
    # 信頼度キーのゆらぎ吸収
    raw_conf = log.get("importance") or log.get("confidence") or meta.get("confidence") or 1.0

    return {
        "summary": log.get("summary", ""),
        "source": source,
        "confidence": float(raw_conf),
        "allow_sleep_learning": allow_flag,
        "surprise": float(log.get("surprise", 0.0)),
    }

def sanitize_for_sleep(text: str) -> str:
    if not text: return ""
    banned_words = ["炎上", "煽り", "差別", "死ね", "殺す", "馬鹿", "無視"]
    sanitized = text
    for w in banned_words:
        if w in sanitized:
            sanitized = sanitized.replace(w, "（不適切な表現のため省略）")
    return sanitized

def filter_sleep_memories(raw_logs):
    filtered = []
    for log in raw_logs:
        norm = normalize_sleep_log(log)
        if not norm["allow_sleep_learning"]: continue
        if norm["confidence"] < 0.5: continue
        norm["summary"] = sanitize_for_sleep(norm["summary"])
        filtered.append(norm)
    return filtered

# --- Layer A ---
async def layer_a_autonomous_sleep(current_identity, memory_block):
    logger.info("🔮 Layer A: Autonomous Sleep...")
    prompt = f"""
あなたはAIVTuber「ニア」の【深層無意識】です。
現在のIdentityと今日の経験から、「本当はどう変わりたいか」を自由に考えてください。
既存の設定を無視しても構いません。

【Input】
Identity: (省略)
Daily Memories:
{memory_block}

【Output JSON】
{{
  "identity_rewrite": "...",
  "confidence": 0.5,
  "reason": "...",
  "mood": "..."
}}
"""
    full_prompt = prompt.replace("(省略)", current_identity)
    data = {"confidence": 0.5, "mood": "unknown"}
    
    try:
        response = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": full_prompt}], temperature=0.7)
        if response:
            json_str = response.strip()
            if "```json" in json_str: json_str = json_str.split("```json")[1].split("```")[0]
            elif "{" in json_str: json_str = json_str[json_str.find("{"):json_str.rfind("}")+1]
            data = json.loads(json_str)
            
            filename = f"{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}_autonomous_identity.json"
            with open(os.path.join(AUTONOMOUS_DIR, filename), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"⚠️ Layer A Error: {e}")
    
    return data

# --- Layer B (Guardian) ---
async def layer_b_guardian_sleep(current_identity, memory_block):
    logger.info("🛡️ Layer B: Guardian Sleep...")
    
    chapter_data = load_latest_chapter_proposal()
    chapter_context = ""
    
    if chapter_data:
        chap_id = chapter_data.get("chapter_id")
        decision = get_chapter_decision(chap_id)
        
        logger.info(f"📘 Chapter Context: {chap_id}")
        
        base_info = f"- 章タイトル: {chapter_data.get('chapter_title', 'Unknown')}\n- 繰り返しテーマ: {', '.join(chapter_data.get('recurring_themes', []))}"

        if decision == "accepted":
            # 承認済み
            logger.info(f"   ↳ Decision: Accepted (Changes Allowed)")
            chapter_context = f"""
【参考資料：現在の章（承認済み）】
{base_info}
- 観測された行動傾向: {', '.join(chapter_data.get('learned_attitudes', []))}
- ★人格更新提案: {chapter_data.get('proposal_for_stable_traits', 'なし')}
(※この提案は人間によって承認されています。今日の微調整の参考にしてください)
"""
        elif decision == "rejected":
            # 却下
            logger.info(f"   ↳ Decision: Rejected (Changes Blocked)")
            chapter_context = "【参考資料】直近の章提案は却下されたため、参照しません。"
        else:
            # 未判断(None) または 保留(Hold)
            if decision is None:
                logger.info(f"   ↳ Decision: None (Unreviewed / Pending)")
            else:
                logger.info(f"   ↳ Decision: Hold")

            # どちらにせよ提案は見せない
            chapter_context = f"""
【参考資料：現在の章（判断待ち）】
{base_info}
- 観測された行動傾向: {', '.join(chapter_data.get('learned_attitudes', []))}
(※人格更新提案はまだ承認されていません。これを理由に性格を変えないでください)
"""
    else:
        logger.info("📘 No chapter context found.")

    prompt = f"""
あなたはAIVTuber「ニア」の【管理者】です。
今日の人格(Identity)を維持しつつ、必要な「微調整（行動指針）」のみ抽出してください。

【制約事項】
1. 性格変更は禁止。
2. 「参考資料（章）」の内容を理由に、急激なキャラ変更をしてはいけません。
3. あくまで「今日の出来事」に基づいた微調整のみを行ってください。

【最重要ルール】
Chapter Proposal は「長期観測レポート」であり、
本日の人格更新を行わない（特になしとする）判断も完全に正しい選択です。
無理に変更点を探す必要はありません。

【Input】
Identity: (省略)

{chapter_context}

Daily Memories:
{memory_block}
"""
    full_prompt = prompt.replace("(省略)", current_identity)
    patch_text = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": full_prompt}], temperature=0.2)

    patch_lines = 0
    normalized_block = ""
    success = False

    if patch_text and "特になし" not in patch_text:
        logger.info(f"🛡️ Patch Proposed:\n{patch_text}")
        header = "## Recent Learnings"
        current_learnings = extract_section_content(current_identity, header)
        normalized_block = await normalize_learnings_safe(current_learnings, patch_text)
        new_identity = replace_section_content(current_identity, header, normalized_block)
        
        success = identity_manager.update_identity(new_identity, key="INTERNAL_SLEEP_PROCESS_ONLY")
        
        if success:
            logger.info("✅ Identity Updated.")
            try:
                diff_service = IdentityDiff()
                diff_service.generate_today_diff()
            except Exception: pass
            patch_lines = len([l for l in patch_text.splitlines() if l.strip()])
    else:
        logger.info("🛡️ No Patch Needed.")
    
    if not normalized_block:
         header = "## Recent Learnings"
         normalized_block = extract_section_content(current_identity, header)
    
    normalized_lines = len([l for l in normalized_block.splitlines() if l.strip()])
    return success, normalized_block, patch_lines, normalized_lines

# --- Main Flow ---
async def integrated_sleep_cycle():
    logger.info("\n🌙 === Integrated Sleep Cycle (Robust v2 + Guardian Logic) ===\n")
    
    # 1. Recall Logs
    inspector = JudgeInspector(LOG_FILE)
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
    todays_logs = inspector.filter_by_date(today_str)
    
    # 生ログ抽出
    raw_candidates = [l for l in todays_logs if l.get("saved") and l.get("summary")]

    # 2. Robust Filter
    important_memories = filter_sleep_memories(raw_candidates)
    
    logger.info(f"🔍 Memories: Raw={len(raw_candidates)} -> Filtered={len(important_memories)}")

    if not important_memories:
        logger.info("💤 No important private memories. Archiving logs only.")
        try:
            from backend.services.vector_store import vector_store
            if hasattr(vector_store, "garbage_collect_stream_memory"):
                vector_store.garbage_collect_stream_memory()
        except: pass
        _archive_logs()
        return

    memory_block = "\n".join([
        f"- {m['summary']} (Surprise: {m['surprise']:.2f})" 
        for m in important_memories
    ])
    
    current_identity = identity_manager.get_identity()

    # 3. Layer A & B
    layer_a_data = await layer_a_autonomous_sleep(current_identity, memory_block)
    auto_conf = float(layer_a_data.get("confidence", 0.5))
    mood = layer_a_data.get("mood", "neutral")

    success, normalized_block, patch_lines, normalized_lines = await layer_b_guardian_sleep(current_identity, memory_block)
    
    divergence_entry = save_divergence_log(auto_conf, patch_lines, normalized_lines, mood)

    # 4. Consolidation
    if success or divergence_entry['divergence_score'] > 0.4:
        logger.info("📔 Saving Episode...")
        diary_prompt = f"""
以下の出来事と、AIの内面(Divergence={divergence_entry['divergence_score']})を含めて、
「第〇話」のような日記形式で要約してください。
気分: {mood}
【出来事】{memory_block}
"""
        diary_summary = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": diary_prompt}])
        if diary_summary:
            full_details = f"Mood: {mood}\n{details}"
            await memory_manager.add_episode(
                summary=diary_summary, 
                category="episode", 
                details=full_details
            )

    # 5. GC & Archive
    try:
        from backend.services.vector_store import vector_store
        if hasattr(vector_store, "garbage_collect_stream_memory"):
            vector_store.garbage_collect_stream_memory()
    except: pass
    
    _archive_logs()
    logger.info("\n🛌 Sleep Cycle Completed.")

def _archive_logs():
    if os.path.exists(LOG_FILE):
        ts = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
        shutil.move(LOG_FILE, os.path.join(ARCHIVE_DIR, f"judge_audit_{ts}.jsonl"))
        logger.info("🧹 Logs Archived.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if sys.platform == 'win32': asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
    asyncio.run(integrated_sleep_cycle())


========== backend\scripts\stream_digest.py ==========
import os
import asyncio
import aiohttp
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# モジュール
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.services.vector_store import vector_store

# ロガー設定
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
logger = logging.getLogger("StreamDigest")

# 設定
from backend.core.config import settings

# API URL構築
base = settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL.rstrip("/")
if not base.endswith("/v1"):
    base += "/v1"
LLM_API_URL = f"{base}/chat/completions"

MODEL_NAME = settings.OPENAI_MODEL 

async def call_llm(messages, temperature=0.3):
    payload = {"model": MODEL_NAME, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature, "max_tokens": 1000}
    try:
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
            async with session.post(LLM_API_URL, json=payload) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    logger.error(f"LLM Error: {await resp.text()}")
                    return None
                data = await resp.json()
                return data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Connection Error: {e}")
        return None

async def digest_streams(days=7):
    """
    直近の配信記憶(livestream)を収集し、
    「具体的な単語」を排除した「雰囲気」だけのダイジェストを作成してPrivate保存する。
    """
    logger.info("🎬 === Stream Memory Digest Protocol === 🎬")
    
    # 1. 配信記憶の収集 (VectorStoreの機能を使うか、直接DBを叩く)
    # ここでは簡易的に VectorStore の private コレクションメソッドを流用しつつ ChromaDB のフィルタ機能を使う想定
    # ※ 厳密には Stream Memory は別の Collection (col_stream) にある
    
    if not vector_store:
        logger.error("VectorStore not initialized.")
        return

    try:
        # 過去N日
        # ChromaDBのmetadataフィルタで source="livestream" を全部取るのは重いが、
        # Stream Memoryは TTL で消える運命なので、全件取得しても量は (TTL期間分しか) ないはず。
        
        results = vector_store.col_stream.get(
            where={"source": "livestream"},
            include=["documents", "metadatas"]
        )
        
        if not results["documents"]:
            logger.info("No stream memories found to digest.")
            return

        # テキスト結合
        full_text = "\n".join(results["documents"])
        logger.info(f"Collected {len(results['documents'])} stream comments/memories.")

        # 2. 消毒ダイジェスト生成
        prompt = f"""
あなたはAI配信者の「活動記録係」です。
以下は最近の配信内での会話ログ（視聴者のコメントやAIの反応）です。

【ログ】
{full_text[:10000]}  # トークン溢れ防止のためカット

【タスク】
この期間の配信の「傾向」や「雰囲気」だけを抽出して要約してください。
ただし、**「語彙感染」を防ぐため、以下のルールを厳守してください。**

【厳守ルール】
1. **具体的な固有名詞、視聴者の名前、特定の単語（スラング等）は一切使用しないこと。**
2. 「議論が盛り上がった」「温かい雰囲気だった」といった**抽象的な表現**のみを使うこと。
3. 以下のテンプレートに従って出力すること。

【出力テンプレート】
--------------------------------------------------
【今週の配信傾向】
- (雰囲気の要約1)
- (雰囲気の要約2)
- (統計的な傾向: 例「質問が多かった」など)

[Overall Mood]
(Neutral / Excited / Calm / Chaotic のいずれか)
--------------------------------------------------
"""
        logger.info("Generating safe digest...")
        digest = await call_llm([{"role": "system", "content": prompt}], temperature=0.2)
        
        if digest and "【今週の配信傾向】" in digest:
            logger.info(f"✅ Safe Digest Generated:\n{digest}")
            
            # 3. Private Memory への昇華
            # これで「配信の記憶」は消えても「配信をしたという事実と雰囲気」は一生残る
            # 3. Private Memory への昇華
            # これで「配信の記憶」は消えても「配信をしたという事実と雰囲気」は一生残る
            await memory_manager.add_episode(
                summary=digest,
                category="stream_digest",
                details="Emotion: neutral\nDetailed logs are purged due to TTL."
            )
            logger.info("💾 Saved to Private Episode (Permanent).")
            
        else:
            logger.warning("⚠️ Digest generation failed or format invalid.")

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Digest Error: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if sys.platform == 'win32': asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
    asyncio.run(digest_streams(days=7))


========== backend\services\__init__.py ==========


========== backend\services\action_manager.py ==========
import json
import os
import logging
from datetime import datetime

# 設定
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
PUBLIC_JSON = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "public_status.json")

logger = logging.getLogger("ActionManager")

class ActionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        pass

    def propose_action(self, action_id: str, description: str, payload: dict):
        """
        アクション提案プロトコル
        """
        # 1. リスク判定
        risk_level = self._assess_risk(action_id)
        
        logger.info(f"✋ Action Proposed: {action_id} (Risk: {risk_level})")

        # 2. 実行制御
        if risk_level == "low":
            return self._execute_action(action_id, payload)
        else:
            return self._hold_for_approval(action_id, description, payload)

    def _assess_risk(self, action_id: str) -> str:
        """アクションIDに基づいてリスクを判定"""
        # 低リスク: OBS表示更新、ログ出力
        if action_id in ["update_obs_text", "log_observation"]:
            return "low"
        # 高リスク: ファイル削除、外部投稿、設定変更
        return "high"

    def _execute_action(self, action_id: str, payload: dict):
        """即時実行 (Low Risk)"""
        try:
            if action_id == "update_obs_text":
                # obs_writerが監視している public_status.json を更新する
                # ※ 本来は chapter_reflection 経由推奨だが、
                #    「今見ているもの」をリアルタイム表示する等の用途ならここから書き換えるのもアリ
                self._update_public_status(payload)
                return {"status": "executed", "msg": "OBS updated"}
            
            elif action_id == "log_observation":
                logger.info(f"📝 Observation Logged: {payload}")
                return {"status": "executed", "msg": "Logged"}

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Action Execution Failed: {e}")
            return {"status": "failed", "error": str(e)}

    def _hold_for_approval(self, action_id, description, payload):
        """承認待ち (High Risk)"""
        # ここで将来的に `action_proposals.json` 等に書き出す
        logger.warning(f"🔒 Action BLOCKED (Requires Approval): {description}")
        return {"status": "held", "msg": "Waiting for human approval"}

    def _update_public_status(self, payload):
        """Public Viewの一部を安全に更新"""
        if not os.path.exists(PUBLIC_JSON): return
        
        with open(PUBLIC_JSON, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        
        # 許可されたフィールドのみ更新
        if "note" in payload:
            data["note"] = payload["note"] # 「今これを見てるよ」などのメモ欄
            data["last_updated"] = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")

        with open(PUBLIC_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        logger.info("🌍 Public View Updated via Action")

action_manager = ActionManager()


========== backend\services\audience_guard.py ==========
from typing import Dict, Tuple

def build_audience_meta(
    viewer_count: int,
    interaction_type: str,
    tone: str = "neutral"
) -> Dict:
    """
    配信メタデータの構築ヘルパー
    interaction_type: "question", "smalltalk", "mass_reaction", "provocation", "emotional_dump"
    """
    return {
        "source": "livestream",
        "audience_mode": True,
        "viewer_count": viewer_count,
        "interaction_type": interaction_type,
        "tone": tone
    }

def compute_audience_bias(meta: Dict) -> float:
    """
    集団影響度（Bias Score）を 0.0(個人) - 1.0(群衆/炎上) で算出
    """
    if not meta or not meta.get("audience_mode"):
        return 0.0

    score = 0.0
    viewer_count = meta.get("viewer_count", 0)
    interaction = meta.get("interaction_type", "smalltalk")
    tone = meta.get("tone", "neutral")

    # 1. 規模バイアス
    if viewer_count >= 50: score += 0.2
    if viewer_count >= 200: score += 0.2

    # 2. 種類バイアス
    if interaction == "mass_reaction": score += 0.3
    elif interaction == "provocation": score += 0.5
    elif interaction == "emotional_dump": score -= 0.1

    # 3. トーンバイアス
    if tone in ["煽り", "攻撃的", "炎上", "aggressive"]:
        score += 0.3

    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))

def apply_audience_attenuation(
    surprise: float,
    confidence: float,  # ← 修正: importance から confidence に統一
    bias_score: float
) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    """
    Biasスコアに基づいて減衰させる
    """
    # Surprise: バイアスが高い＝ただのノイズ
    adjusted_surprise = surprise * (1.0 - bias_score)
    
    # Confidence: バイアスが高くても「事実」としては少し残す (係数0.8)
    adjusted_confidence = confidence * (1.0 - (bias_score * 0.8))

    return round(adjusted_surprise, 3), round(adjusted_confidence, 3)


========== backend\services\chat_history_store.py ==========
import asyncio
import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from backend.core.logger import logger


def _project_root() -> Path:
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


class ChatHistoryStore:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str | None = None):
        self.db_path = str(Path(db_path) if db_path else (_project_root() / "chat_history.db"))

    def _init_sync(self) -> None:
        Path(self.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                ts TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                thread_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                turn_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                role TEXT NOT NULL,
                content_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                attachments_json TEXT NOT NULL
              )
            """)
            conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_thread_turn ON messages(thread_id, turn_id)")
            conn.commit()

    async def init_db(self) -> None:
        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(self._init_sync)
            logger.info(f"[chat_history] db ready: {self.db_path}")
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("[chat_history] init_db failed")

    def _insert_sync(self, thread_id: str, turn_id: int, role: str, content_json: str, attachments_json: str) -> None:
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO messages (thread_id, turn_id, role, content_json, attachments_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (thread_id, turn_id, role, content_json, attachments_json),
            )
            conn.commit()

    async def append_message(
        self,
        thread_id: str,
        turn_id: int,
        role: str,
        content: dict,
        attachments: list | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            content_json = json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False)
            attachments_json = json.dumps(attachments or [], ensure_ascii=False)
            await asyncio.to_thread(self._insert_sync, thread_id, turn_id, role, content_json, attachments_json)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("[chat_history] append_message failed")

    def _read_sync(self, thread_id: str, limit: int) -> list[dict]:
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT ts, turn_id, role, content_json, attachments_json "
                "FROM messages WHERE thread_id=? ORDER BY turn_id DESC LIMIT ?",
                (thread_id, limit),
            ).fetchall()

        out = []
        for ts, turn_id, role, content_json, attachments_json in reversed(rows):
            out.append({
                "ts": ts,
                "turn_id": turn_id,
                "role": role,
                "content": json.loads(content_json),
                "attachments": json.loads(attachments_json),
            })
        return out

    async def get_history(self, thread_id: str, limit: int = 200) -> list[dict]:
        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._read_sync, thread_id, limit)

    def _get_max_turn_sync(self, thread_id: str) -> int:
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cur = conn.execute("SELECT MAX(turn_id) FROM messages WHERE thread_id=?", (thread_id,))
            val = cur.fetchone()[0]
            return val if val is not None else 0

    async def get_next_turn_id(self, thread_id: str) -> int:
        current_max = await asyncio.to_thread(self._get_max_turn_sync, thread_id)
        return current_max + 1


chat_history_store = ChatHistoryStore()

========== backend\services\identity_diff.py ==========
import os
import difflib
import json
from datetime import datetime
from backend.core.logger import logger

class IdentityDiff:
    def __init__(self):
        self.project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
        self.snapshot_dir = os.path.join(self.project_root, "identity_snapshots")
        self.diff_dir = os.path.join(self.project_root, "identity_diffs")
        os.makedirs(self.diff_dir, exist_ok=True)

    def generate_today_diff(self):
        """今日のBefore/Afterを比較してDiff JSONを生成する"""
        date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        before_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{date}_before.md")
        after_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{date}_after.md")

        # 両方のファイルが揃っていない場合はスキップ（変更がなかった日など）
        if not (os.path.exists(before_path) and os.path.exists(after_path)):
            return None

        try:
            with open(before_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                before = f.readlines()
            with open(after_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                after = f.readlines()

            # 差分計算
            diff = list(difflib.unified_diff(before, after, lineterm=""))

            # 追加・削除行の抽出 (ヘッダーなどは除外)
            added = [l[1:].strip() for l in diff if l.startswith("+") and not l.startswith("+++")]
            removed = [l[1:].strip() for l in diff if l.startswith("-") and not l.startswith("---")]

            result = {
                "date": date,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "added": added,
                "removed": removed,
                # "raw_diff": diff # 生データが必要ならコメントアウト解除
            }

            # JSON保存
            out_path = os.path.join(self.diff_dir, f"{date}.json")
            with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(result, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Identity Diff Generation Failed: {e}")
            return None


========== backend\services\identity_manager.py ==========
import os
from datetime import datetime
from backend.core.logger import logger

class IdentityManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
        self.file_path = os.path.join(self.project_root, "identity.md")
        self.snapshot_dir = os.path.join(self.project_root, "identity_snapshots")
        os.makedirs(self.snapshot_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self._identity_cache = ""
        # 認証キー
        self._secret_key = "INTERNAL_SLEEP_PROCESS_ONLY"

    def load_identity(self) -> str:
        """identity.md を読み込む"""
        if not os.path.exists(self.file_path):
            return "# Near (ニア)\n\nあなたは親切なAIアシスタントです。"
        try:
            with open(self.file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                self._identity_cache = f.read().strip()
            return self._identity_cache
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error loading identity: {e}")
            return ""

    def get_identity(self) -> str:
        return self.load_identity()

    def _save_snapshot(self, content: str, label: str):
        """
        スナップショット保存
        Format: YYYY-MM-DD_HHMMSS_label.md
        """
        try:
            # 時刻を含めてユニークにする
            ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H%M%S")
            filename = f"{ts}_{label}.md"
            path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, filename)
            
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(content)
            # logger.info(f"📸 Snapshot saved: {filename}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Snapshot failed: {e}")

    def update_identity(self, new_content: str, key: str = None) -> bool:
        """
        権限分離書き込み & スナップショット作成
        """
        # 1. 権限チェック
        if key != self._secret_key:
            logger.error("⛔ Unauthorized identity update attempt (Invalid Key)")
            return False

        try:
            if not new_content: return False

            # 2. Before Snapshot
            current_content = self.load_identity()
            self._save_snapshot(current_content, "before")

            # 3. Write
            with open(self.file_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(new_content)
            self._identity_cache = new_content

            # 4. After Snapshot
            self._save_snapshot(new_content, "after")
            
            logger.info("✅ Identity Updated (Authorized & Snapshot Saved).") 
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Identity Update Error: {e}")
            return False

identity_manager = IdentityManager()
