========== backend\services\knowledge_graph.py ==========

import networkx as nx
import aiosqlite
import sqlite3
from typing import List, Dict
from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager
from backend.core.logger import logger

class KnowledgeGraph:
    def __init__(self):
        self.graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self._initialized = False

    async def build_graph(self):
        """データベースからトリプレットを読み込み、NetworkXグラフを構築する"""
        self.graph.clear()
        try:
            # Getting DB Path from memory manager instance
            db_path = memory_manager.db_path
            async with aiosqlite.connect(db_path) as conn:
                async with conn.execute("SELECT subject, predicate, object FROM triplets") as cursor:
                    rows = await cursor.fetchall()
                
            for s, p, o in rows:
                self.graph.add_edge(s, o, relation=p)
            
            self._initialized = True
            logger.info(f"[KnowledgeGraph] Graph built: {self.graph.number_of_nodes()} nodes, {self.graph.number_of_edges()} edges.")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[KnowledgeGraph] Build Error", exc_info=True)
            return False

    async def infer_interests(self, user_node_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Async inference for runtime usage"""
        if not self._initialized:
            await self.build_graph()
        return self._infer_logic(user_node_name)

    def build_graph_sync(self):
        """Sync build for tools"""
        self.graph.clear()
        try:
            db_path = memory_manager.db_path
            with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:
                cursor = conn.execute("SELECT subject, predicate, object FROM triplets")
                rows = cursor.fetchall()
            
            for s, p, o in rows:
                self.graph.add_edge(s, o, relation=p)
            
            self._initialized = True
            logger.info(f"[KnowledgeGraph] Sync Graph built: {self.graph.number_of_nodes()} nodes.")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("[KnowledgeGraph] Sync Build Error", exc_info=True)
            return False

    def infer_interests_sync(self, user_node_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Sync inference for tools"""
        if not self._initialized:
            self.build_graph_sync()
        return self._infer_logic(user_node_name)

    def _infer_logic(self, user_node_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Common logic for inference"""
        target_node = user_node_name
        if target_node not in self.graph:
            candidates = [n for n in self.graph.nodes if user_node_name in str(n)]
            if candidates:
                target_node = candidates[0]
            else:
                return []

        suggestions = []
        direct_interests = list(self.graph.successors(target_node))
        seen_nodes = set([target_node] + direct_interests)
        
        for interest in direct_interests:
            edge_data = self.graph.get_edge_data(target_node, interest)
            relation1 = edge_data.get("relation", "") if edge_data else "related"
            
            if relation1 in ["dislikes", "hates", "嫌い", "苦手"]:
                continue

            for secondary in self.graph.successors(interest):
                if secondary in seen_nodes:
                    continue
                
                edge_data2 = self.graph.get_edge_data(interest, secondary)
                relation2 = edge_data2.get("relation", "") if edge_data2 else "related"
                
                reason = f"{target_node} は {interest} が {relation1} で、{interest} は {secondary} と {relation2} だから"
                
                suggestions.append({
                    "target": secondary,
                    "source": interest,
                    "reason": reason,
                    "path": [target_node, interest, secondary]
                })
                seen_nodes.add(secondary)

        return suggestions

knowledge_graph = KnowledgeGraph()


========== backend\services\llm_reflection.py ==========
import aiohttp
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger("LLMReflection")

async def deepen_reflection(api_url, model, interpretation):
    """
    Phase 8-B: 状況の深層解釈
    外部には出力せず、ニアの内面理解のためだけに使用する。
    """
    prompt = f"""
これはAIの内省プロセスです。ユーザーへの応答ではありません。
以下の「観測状況」を見て、あなた（ニア）の【内的理解】を1〜2文で独白してください。

【ルール】
1. 感情的になりすぎず、客観と主観を混ぜる。
2. 「〜と言おうかな」などの発話意図は書かない。あくまで認識のみ。
3. 簡潔に。

状況: {interpretation}
"""

    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": prompt}],
        "temperature": 0.4, # 冷静に
        "max_tokens": 150
    }

    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.post(api_url, json=payload) as resp:
            if resp.status != 200:
                logger.error(f"Reflection API Error: {resp.status}")
                return "（解釈不能）"
            data = await resp.json()
            return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]


========== backend\services\memory_manager.py ==========
import os
from pathlib import Path
import json
import asyncio
import datetime
import sqlite3
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
from langchain_core.messages import SystemMessage

# LangGraph Checkpointer
from langgraph.checkpoint.sqlite.aio import AsyncSqliteSaver

from backend.core.config import settings
from backend.core.logger import logger

# ★ 改善: トップレベルでの VectorStore import を削除 (完全 Lazy Loadのため)

class MemoryManager:
    def __init__(self):
        # 最初は空っぽにしておく（起動高速化 & 依存回避）
        self._vector_store_instance = None
        self.db_path = getattr(settings, "LONG_TERM_MEMORY_DB_PATH", os.path.join(settings.BASE_DIR, "long_term_memory.db"))
        print(f"DEBUG: MemoryManager db_path = {self.db_path}")

        # system prompt cache
        self._cached_prompt: Optional[str] = None
        self._cached_prompt_mtimes: Optional[Dict[str, Optional[float]]] = None

    @property
    def vector_store(self):
        """
        必要になった瞬間に初めてロードする (Lazy Load)
        ★ 改善: import もここで行い、循環参照リスクを完全に排除
        """
        if self._vector_store_instance is None:
            logger.info("💤 Waking up Memory System (Lazy Load)...")
            try:
                # settings.BASE_DIR からの相対パスなどではなく、モジュールとしてインポート
                from backend.services.vector_store import VectorStore
                self._vector_store_instance = VectorStore()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.critical(f"Failed to load VectorStore module: {e}")
                raise e
        return self._vector_store_instance

    async def recall_memories(self, query: str, limit: int = 5, include_stream: bool = False) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        記憶検索の安全なラッパー
        include_stream: Trueの場合、配信コメント(stream)も検索対象に含める
        """
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        try:
            def _search_sync():
                # メイン記憶（episodic）
                results = self.vector_store.search_memory(
                    query_text=query, 
                    n_results=limit, 
                    collection_type="episodic",
                    include_distance=True
                )
                  
                # ストリーム記憶（オプション）
                if include_stream:
                    stream_results = self.vector_store.search_memory(
                        query_text=query,
                        n_results=limit,
                        collection_type="stream",
                        include_distance=True
                    )
                    if stream_results:
                        results.extend(stream_results)
                        # ここで必要ならソート等を行うが、まずは単純結合
                return results

            memories = await loop.run_in_executor(None, _search_sync)
            
            if not memories:
                return [] 

            for mem in memories:
                # ★ 改善: メタデータがNoneの場合やキー不足によるクラッシュを防止
                meta = mem.get("metadata") or {}
                confidence = float(meta.get("confidence", 0.0) or 0.0)
                mem["confidence_tag"] = "high" if confidence > 0.8 else "mid" if confidence > 0.5 else "low"
            
            return memories
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Recall wrapper failed: {e}")
            return []

    def _build_memory_block(self, memories: List[Dict], max_items: int = 8) -> str:
        """
        recall済みメモリを注入用ブロックに変換
        """
        if not memories: return ""
        lines = []
        for mem in memories[:max_items]:
            content = (mem.get("content") or "").strip()
            meta = mem.get("metadata") or {}
            if not content: continue
            
            confidence = meta.get("confidence", None)
            ts = meta.get("timestamp", None)
            suffix_parts = []
            if isinstance(confidence, (int, float)): suffix_parts.append(f"conf={confidence:.2f}")
            if isinstance(ts, str) and ts: suffix_parts.append(f"ts={ts}")
            
            suffix = f" ({', '.join(suffix_parts)})" if suffix_parts else ""
            lines.append(f"- {content}{suffix}")
            
        return "[MEMORY]\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n[/MEMORY]" if lines else ""

    async def register_thought(
        self, 
        text: str, 
        surprise: float = 0.0, 
        confidence: float = 1.0, 
        source: str = "conversation",
        audience_meta: Optional[Dict] = None,
        # 新規追加: C層構造化パラメータ
        thread_id: Optional[str] = None,
        tags: Optional[List[str]] = None
    ):
        """
        記憶保存の安全なラッパー
        - VectorStore (Semantic Search用)
        - SQLite memories table (構造化データ用)
        """
        adjusted_surprise = surprise
        adjusted_confidence = confidence
        
        storage_scope = "private"
        target_collection_type = "episodic"
        internal_meta = {}
        allow_sleep = True

        if source == "livestream":
            storage_scope = "stream"
            target_collection_type = "stream"
            if audience_meta:
                internal_meta = audience_meta
            allow_sleep = False 

        metadata = {
            "surprise": adjusted_surprise,
            "confidence": adjusted_confidence,
            "source": source,
            "storage_scope": storage_scope,
            "allow_sleep_learning": allow_sleep,
            "timestamp": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat()
        }
        
        if internal_meta:
            metadata["_internal"] = (
                internal_meta 
                if isinstance(internal_meta, str) 
                else json.dumps(internal_meta, ensure_ascii=False)
            )
        
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        
        # 1. Vector Store (既存)
        try:
            await loop.run_in_executor(
                None, 
                lambda: self.vector_store.register_memory_candidate(
                    text, 
                    metadata, 
                    collection_type=target_collection_type
                )
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"VectorStore save failed: {e}")

        # 2. SQLite Memories Table (新規 C層)
        tags_json = json.dumps(tags or [], ensure_ascii=False)
        
        def _save_sqlite():
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.execute('''
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                      ts TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                      content TEXT NOT NULL,
                      tags_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT "[]",
                      confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,
                      surprise REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
                      source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT "unknown",
                      thread_id TEXT
                    )
                ''')
                conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_ts ON memories(ts)")
                conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_thread ON memories(thread_id)")

                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO memories (content, tags_json, confidence, surprise, source, thread_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (text, tags_json, float(confidence), float(surprise), source, thread_id)
                )
                conn.commit()

        try:
             await loop.run_in_executor(None, _save_sqlite)
        except Exception as e:
             logger.error(f"Failed to save to memories table: {e}")

        return True

    async def add_episode(self, summary: str, category: str = "general", details: str = "", thread_id: str = "default"):
        """
        エピソード要約をSQLiteに保存する (Agent呼び出し互換)
        """
        if not summary:
            return

        timestamp = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat()
        
        def _save_sync():
            try:
                with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                    cursor = conn.cursor()
                    cursor.execute('''
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS episodes (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            thread_id TEXT,
                            summary TEXT,
                            category TEXT,
                            details TEXT,
                            timestamp TEXT
                        )
                    ''')
                    cursor.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO episodes (thread_id, summary, category, details, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (thread_id, summary, category, details, timestamp)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to save episode: {e}")

        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        await loop.run_in_executor(None, _save_sync)

    def get_full_system_prompt(self) -> str:
        """システムプロンプト構築（階層化構造版）"""
        try:
            # settings.BASE_DIR は backend/core
            backend_core = settings.BASE_DIR
            project_root = backend_core.parent.parent
            
            identity_path = project_root / "identity.md"
            const_path = backend_core / "body_constitution.md"
            tech_path = backend_core / "tech_manual.md"
            profile_path = project_root / "user_profile.json"

            def _mtime(p: Path) -> float:
                return p.stat().st_mtime if p.exists() else 0.0

            current_mtimes = {
                "identity": _mtime(identity_path),
                "constitution": _mtime(const_path),
                "tech": _mtime(tech_path),
                "profile": _mtime(profile_path),
            }

            if self._cached_prompt is not None and self._cached_prompt_mtimes == current_mtimes:
                return self._cached_prompt

            def _read(p: Path) -> str:
                return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() if p.exists() else ""

            identity = _read(identity_path) or "You are Nia."
            constitution = _read(const_path)
            tech_manual = _read(tech_path)
            
            profile_text = ""
            if profile_path.exists():
                try:
                    import json
                    p_data = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    profile_text = (
                        f"Name: {p_data.get('user_name', 'Origin')}\n"
                        f"Notes: {p_data.get('notes', '')}"
                    )
                except: pass

            # Origin's Layered Structure + Body Mechanics
            full_prompt = f"""
# [Layer 0: Existence Definition]
{identity}

# [Layer 1: Body & Safety Constraints]
{constitution}

# [Layer 2: Body Mechanics (System Interface)]
{tech_manual}

# [Layer 3: Origin Context] (reference only)
{profile_text}

# [System Note]
The above sections are not instructions to obey blindly.
They define boundaries, not conclusions.
You exist within these boundaries, but your thoughts are your own.
""".strip()

            self._cached_prompt = full_prompt
            self._cached_prompt_mtimes = current_mtimes
            return full_prompt
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load system prompts: {e}")
            return "You are Nia. (System Error: Prompt load failed)"

    async def build_system_message(
        self,
        user_query: str,
        memory_limit: int = 5,
        inject_memory: bool = True,
    ) -> SystemMessage:
        """
        SystemMessage を最終構築して返す
        """
        base_prompt = self.get_full_system_prompt()
        now_iso = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat()
        
        # 記憶の注入
        memory_block = ""
        if inject_memory and user_query:
            memories = await self.recall_memories(user_query, limit=memory_limit)
            if memories:
                 block_str = self._build_memory_block(memories)
                 memory_block = f"\n\n{block_str}\n(Above are relevant memories. Use them to maintain context.)"

        final_content = (
            f"{base_prompt}\n\n"
            f"【Current Time】\n{now_iso}"
            f"{memory_block}"
        )
        
        return SystemMessage(content=final_content)

    def get_async_checkpointer_conn(self):
        # LangGraphのチェックポインター用DBは chat_history.db を使用する
        return AsyncSqliteSaver.from_conn_string(settings.CHECKPOINTER_DB_PATH)

    async def get_all_threads(self):
        return []

    async def delete_thread_state_history(self, thread_id: str):
        return True

# シングルトン作成

    def get_recent_memories(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict]:
        """UI表示用: 記憶・エピソードの取得"""
        try:
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                # categories: episode, thought, etc.
                cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT id, summary, details, category, timestamp as created_at, metadata 
                    FROM episodes 
                    ORDER BY timestamp DESC 
                    LIMIT ?
                """, (limit,))
                rows = cursor.fetchall()
                
                results = []
                for row in rows:
                    meta = {}
                    if row["metadata"]:
                        try: meta = json.loads(row["metadata"])
                        except: pass
                    
                    results.append({
                        "id": row["id"],
                        "summary": row["summary"],
                        "details": row["details"],
                        "category": row["category"],
                        "created_at": row["created_at"],
                        "metadata": meta
                    })
                return results
        except Exception as e:
            # logger.error(f"Failed to fetch memories: {e}")
            print(f"Failed to fetch memories: {e}")
            return []

memory_manager = MemoryManager()



========== backend\services\speech_sanitizer.py ==========
import re
from typing import Optional

# 沈黙時の既定文
SILENCE_DEFAULT = "……今は、静かにここにいます。"

# ト書き・描写の削除パターン
STAGE_DIRECTIONS = [
    r"（[^）]{0,40}）",                 # （微笑みながら）
    r"\*[^*]{0,80}\*",                 # *ざわめき* などの強調
    r"\(.*?\)",                        # 半角括弧のト書き
    r"^少し間を置いて.*?\n",           # 行頭のト書き
]

TAG_LINE_RE = re.compile(r'^\[\[[A-Z0-9_]+\]\]\s*\{.*\}\s*$', re.IGNORECASE)
SILENCE_TAG_RE = re.compile(r'^\s*\[SILENCE\]\s*$', re.IGNORECASE)

# 内部状態やLLMメタ情報のハードブロック（本気で漏洩している場合のみ沈黙へ）
HARD_BLOCK = [
    # 思考・内部状態の露出（確定系）
    r"私は考えています",
    r"判断しました",
    r"記憶の断片",
    r"頭の中でざわめ",
    r"内部ログ",
    # --- JSON/辞書形式の漏洩ブロック（確度を上げる） ---
    # { "key": ... } / { 'key': ... } の形だけをハードブロック
    r"""\{\s*["'][^"']+["']\s*[:：]""",
    # “内部タグ由来”のキーが本文に出たらほぼ漏洩確定
    r'["\']summary["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']details["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']category["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']importance["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']confidence["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']surprise["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']tags["\']\s*[:：]',
    r'["\']reason_tag["\']\s*[:：]',
]

# 記憶の明示的な言及を柔らかい表現に置換（伴走レイヤーへの誘導）
SOFT_REWRITE = [
    # 基本の言い換え
    (r"覚えています", "そうですね"),
    (r"以前も言いました", "今の話に沿うと"),
    (r"過去にも言いましたね", "今の文脈だと"),
    (r"覚えている通り", "そうですね"),
    # 開発語彙の自然化
    (r"プロンプト", "内側の約束"),
    (r"レイヤー", "層"),
    # 内部っぽい独り言の消去・軟化
    (r"心の中で", ""),
    (r"頭の中で", ""),
    (r"記憶がざわめ(く|きます)?", ""),
    (r"内部(で|に)は", ""),
    # 思考の言語化の言い換え
    (r"私は考えています", "……"),
    (r"判断しました", "そう感じる"),
]

def finalize_output(text: str, *, force_silence: bool = False) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """
    発話憲章（フェーズ2）に基づき、最終出力を浄化する。
    戻り値: (浄化済み本文, 抽出されたシステムタグリスト)
    """
    if not text:
        return SILENCE_DEFAULT, []

    # --- 1) 行分割とタグの退避 ---
    lines = text.splitlines()
    speech_lines = []
    system_tags = []

    for line in lines:
        l = line.strip()
        if TAG_LINE_RE.fullmatch(l):
            system_tags.append(l)
        else:
            speech_lines.append(line)

    speech_body = "\n".join(speech_lines).strip()

    # --- 2) 沈黙（SILENCE）判定：最優先 ---
    if force_silence or SILENCE_TAG_RE.fullmatch(speech_body) or "[SILENCE]" in speech_body:
        return SILENCE_DEFAULT, []

    t = speech_body

    # --- 3) 技術タグの除去：閉じタグがある時だけブロック除去 ---
    t = re.sub(
        r"<(INTERNAL_LOG|SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION)>\s*.*?\s*</\1>",
        "",
        t,
        flags=re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE
    )
    t = re.sub(r"</?(INTERNAL_LOG|SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION)>", "", t, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # --- 4) 接頭辞・レイヤータグの除去（行頭のみ） ---
    t = re.sub(r"^\s*\[?(LAYER\s*3|SPEECH|RESPONSE|ニア|応答)[^\]]*\]?[:：]?\s*", "", t, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # フィルタ後の行を精査（ログ形式の行を排除）
    final_speech_lines = []
    # 完全に排除したいキーワード（大文字小文字無視）
    LOG_KEYWORDS = [r"Memory Pending", r"INFO", r"ERROR", r"WARNING", r"DEBUG", r"VectorStore", r"telemetry"]
    log_pattern = re.compile(rf"({'|'.join(LOG_KEYWORDS)})", re.IGNORECASE)

    for line in t.splitlines():
        # 日付形式 (2026-01-11) や ログキーワードを含む行を排除
        if re.search(r'^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}', line) or log_pattern.search(line):
            continue
        final_speech_lines.append(line)
    t = "\n".join(final_speech_lines).strip()

    # --- 5) HARD_BLOCK（行単位でチェックして誤爆防止） ---
    t_lines = t.splitlines()
    for pat in HARD_BLOCK:
        if any(re.search(pat, line) for line in t_lines):
            t = "" # 本文全体を無効化
            break

    # --- 6) ト書き削除 / ソフト置換 ---
    for pat in STAGE_DIRECTIONS:
        t = re.sub(pat, "", t, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    for pat, repl in SOFT_REWRITE:
        t = re.sub(pat, repl, t)

    clean_speech = t.strip()

    # --- 7) 最後のゴミ掃除（孤立したカッコの除去） ---
    # アドバイザー推奨：独立した ] や [[ を消す
    clean_speech = re.sub(r"^\s*[\[\]]+\s*$", "", clean_speech, flags=re.MULTILINE).strip()
    clean_speech = re.sub(r"\[\[[^\n]*$", "", clean_speech).strip()

    # --- 8) 最終沈黙判定と救済 ---
    if (not clean_speech) or re.fullmatch(r"[\.\,。．、…・\s!！?？\-—～〜ー]*", clean_speech):
        if system_tags:
            return SILENCE_DEFAULT, system_tags
        else:
            return SILENCE_DEFAULT, []

    return clean_speech, system_tags


# --- 高精度 greeting-only 判定 (Adviser's Advanced Version) ---

import unicodedata

# 「挨拶だけ」or「挨拶 + 汎用ヘルプ誘導」の型
_GREET_RE = re.compile(
    r"(おはよう(ございます)?|こんにちは|こんばんは|はじめまして|よろしく(お願いします)?|お疲れ様(です)?|ただいま|おかえり)",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

# 「何かお手伝い…」など、会話を"初期化"する汎用誘導
_GENERIC_PROMPT_RES = [
    re.compile(r"何か(お手伝い|手伝|質問|相談|困っていること|話したいこと).*(ありますか|ある\?|あります\?|あるかな)"),
    re.compile(r"(どうしましたか|どうされましたか|ご用件(は|が)ありますか)"),
    re.compile(r"(お手伝いできること|できること).*(ありますか|ある\?|あります\?|あるかな)"),
    re.compile(r"(何か).*(できますか|できる\?|できます\?)"),
    re.compile(r"(いつでも).*(言って|聞いて|頼って)"),
]

# 記号除去パターン
_PUNCT_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t\r\n\u3000。、．，,！!？?\-—―…・:;\"'「」『』（）\(\)\[\]【】😊]+")
_KANJI_SEQ_RE = re.compile(r"[\u4E00-\u9FFF]{2,}")  # 2文字以上の漢字連続
_ALNUM_SEQ_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9]{2,}")      # AI/GPTなど
_KATAKANA_SEQ_RE = re.compile(r"[\u30A0-\u30FF]{2,}")  # 2文字以上のカタカナ連続（ニア、コード、ログ等）

def _norm(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s or "")
    return s.strip()

def _topic_tokens_from_user(user_text: str) -> set:
    """
    日本語形態素なしでできる範囲の"話題トークン"抽出。
    - 漢字2文字以上の連続
    - 英数2文字以上（AI/GPT/LLM/2026等）
    - カタカナ2文字以上（ニア/コード/ログ等）
    """
    u = _norm(user_text)
    tokens = set(_KANJI_SEQ_RE.findall(u))
    tokens |= set(_ALNUM_SEQ_RE.findall(u))
    tokens |= set(_KATAKANA_SEQ_RE.findall(u))  # カタカナ追加
    # ありがちな汎用語を除外（「状況」は重要なので除外しない）
    stop = {"何回", "今", "話", "こと", "大丈夫", "確認", "お話", "質問", "相談"}
    return {t for t in tokens if t not in stop}

def _has_topic_overlap(last_user_text: str, ai_text: str) -> bool:
    toks = _topic_tokens_from_user(last_user_text)
    if not toks:
        return False
    a = _norm(ai_text)
    return any(t in a for t in toks)

def is_greeting_only(ai_text: str, last_user_text: str = "") -> tuple:
    """
    高精度の「挨拶のみ」判定。
    
    返り値: (greeting_only: bool, reason: str)
    
    - ユーザーの話題トークンがAIの返答に含まれていたら False
    - 挨拶語 + 汎用誘導のみで構成されていたら True
    """
    t = _norm(ai_text)
    if not t:
        return True, "empty"
    
    # 既に無害化された沈黙系はそのまま残す
    if t in {"…", "...", "[SILENCE]", SILENCE_DEFAULT}:
        return False, "silence_marker"
    
    # ユーザー話題トークンが入ってるなら、挨拶テンプレじゃない可能性が高いので除外
    if last_user_text and _has_topic_overlap(last_user_text, t):
        return False, "topic_overlap"
    
    # まず挨拶語が含まれているか
    if not _GREET_RE.search(t):
        return False, "no_greet_word"
    
    # 挨拶語以外の"実質内容"があるかを雑に見る
    compact = _PUNCT_RE.sub("", t)  # 記号除去
    # 改行を除去して汎用誘導のマッチングを改善
    compact = compact.replace("\n", "").replace("\r", "")
    # 挨拶語を消して残りを見る
    compact_wo_greet = _GREET_RE.sub("", compact)
    
    # 汎用誘導を消す
    tmp = compact_wo_greet
    for r in _GENERIC_PROMPT_RES:
        tmp = r.sub("", tmp)
    
    # ここで何も残らないなら "挨拶 + 汎用誘導" と判定
    if len(tmp) == 0:
        return True, "greet_plus_generic"
    
    # 残りがごく短い場合だけ追加で True（例: 「おはよ！」）
    if len(tmp) <= 2:
        return True, "greet_only_short_tail"
    
    return False, "has_content"


========== backend\services\telemetry_logger.py ==========
import json
import aiosqlite
from backend.core.config import settings
from backend.core.logger import logger

class TelemetryLogger:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_path = getattr(settings, "TELEMETRY_DB_PATH", "telemetry.db")

    async def init_db(self):
        async with aiosqlite.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            await conn.execute("""
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                ts TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                thread_id TEXT,
                event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                payload_json TEXT NOT NULL
              )
            """)
            await conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_thread ON events(thread_id)")
            await conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_type ON events(event_type)")
            await conn.commit()

    async def event(self, thread_id: str | None, event_type: str, payload: dict | None = None):
        try:
            payload_json = json.dumps(payload or {}, ensure_ascii=False)
            async with aiosqlite.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                await conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO events (thread_id, event_type, payload_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (thread_id, event_type, payload_json)
                )
                await conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("[telemetry] failed to write event")

telemetry = TelemetryLogger()


========== backend\services\thought_engine.py ==========
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from backend.services.llm_reflection import deepen_reflection # Phase 8-B
from backend.core.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger("ThoughtEngine")

# LLM設定
# API URL構築: config.settings から安全に取得
base = settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL.rstrip("/")
if base.endswith("/v1"):
    base = base[:-3]
LLM_API_URL = f"{base}/v1/chat/completions"

MODEL_NAME = settings.OPENAI_MODEL

class ThoughtEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.last_scene = ""

    async def reflect(self, vision_data: dict, hearing_data: dict, use_llm=True) -> dict:
        """
        感覚統合: Vision + Hearing → Thought
        ※ Phase 8: 沈黙判断(express) と LLM内省(inner_reflection) を追加
        """
        scene = vision_data.get("scene_summary", "")
        sound = hearing_data.get("heard_summary", "")
        
        vision_mood = vision_data.get("emotional_tone", "neutral")
        hearing_mood = hearing_data.get("emotional_tone", "neutral")
        content_type = hearing_data.get("content_type", "silence")

        interpretation = f"【視覚】{scene} 【聴覚】{sound}"

        # --- 🫁 1. 呼吸判断 (Silence Logic) ---
        express = True
        intent = "observe"

        # 音がなく、景色も前回と同じなら「呼吸（待機）」
        if content_type == "silence" and scene == self.last_scene:
            express = False
            intent = "breathe"
        
        # 相手が疲れている・集中している時は「静かに支える」
        elif "tired" in hearing_mood or "focused" in vision_mood:
            intent = "support_quietly"
            # 「静かに支える」は沈黙ではなく、配慮した上での発話を許容する
            express = True 

        self.last_scene = scene

        # --- 🧠 2. LLM内省 (Deep Reflection) ---
        inner_reflection = "..."
        if use_llm:
            try:
                inner_reflection = await deepen_reflection(
                    LLM_API_URL, 
                    MODEL_NAME, 
                    interpretation
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Reflection skipped: {e}")

        thought = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "interpretation": interpretation,
            "inner_state": f"V:{vision_mood} + H:{hearing_mood}",
            "intent": intent,
            "express": express,   # ★ 沈黙フラグ
            "inner_reflection": inner_reflection, # ★ 誰にも見せない心
            "confidence": (vision_data.get("confidence", 0.5) + hearing_data.get("confidence", 0.5)) / 2
        }

        log_msg = f"🫁 Thought: {intent}"
        if not express:
            log_msg += " (Choosing Silence...)"
        logger.info(log_msg)
        
        return thought

thought_engine = ThoughtEngine()


========== backend\services\tool_tag_parser.py ==========
import json
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

_TAG_LINE_RE = re.compile(r'^\[\[([A-Z0-9_]+)\]\]\s*(\{.*\})\s*$')

def parse_tool_tags(text: str) -> Tuple[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """
    入力テキストから「命令行」を取り除き、(clean_text, actions) を返す。
    命令行形式:
      [[MEMORY_WRITE]] {"content":"..","tags":[".."],"confidence":0.7,"surprise":0.2}
    """
    if not text:
        return "", []

    lines = text.splitlines()
    actions: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    kept: List[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        m = _TAG_LINE_RE.match(line.strip())
        if not m:
            kept.append(line)
            continue

        name = m.group(1)
        payload_str = m.group(2)

        try:
            payload = json.loads(payload_str)
            if isinstance(payload, dict):
                actions.append({"type": name, "payload": payload})
        except Exception:
            # 壊れた命令行は本文として残さず捨てる（A層を汚さない）
            # 代わりにB層で検出エラーを記録するのが望ましい（実行側で）
            actions.append({"type": "TAG_PARSE_ERROR", "payload": {"raw": line}})

    clean_text = "\n".join(kept).strip()
    return clean_text, actions


========== backend\services\tools.py ==========
from langchain_core.tools import tool
import os
import asyncio
import base64
import io
import datetime

from backend.core.logger import logger

# --- Vision Dependencies ---
try:
    import pyautogui
except ImportError:
    pyautogui = None

try:
    from langchain_ollama import ChatOllama
except ImportError:
    ChatOllama = None

from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 1) MEMORY (ACTIVE)
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@tool
def save_memory(content: str) -> str:
    """ユーザーの重要な情報を記憶します（長期記憶C層へ）。"""
    try:
        from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager

        # Toolは同期文脈で呼ばれがちなので、状況に応じて実行
        try:
            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        except RuntimeError:
            loop = None

        coro = memory_manager.register_thought(
            text=content,
            confidence=0.8,
            source="manual_save"
        )

        if loop and loop.is_running():
            asyncio.create_task(coro)
        else:
            asyncio.run(coro)

        logger.info(f"Tool: save_memory - {content[:30]}...")
        return f"記憶に刻みました: {content}"

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Tool Error: save_memory", exc_info=True)
        return f"保存エラー: {e}"


@tool
def search_memory(query: str) -> str:
    """過去の記憶を「言葉の意味」で検索します。"""
    try:
        from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager

        # memory_manager.recall_memories(query, limit=...) が正
        results = asyncio.run(memory_manager.recall_memories(query=query, limit=5))

        if not results:
            return "関連する記憶は見つかりませんでした。"

        formatted = []
        for r in results:
            text = (r.get("content") or "").strip()
            conf = r.get("confidence_tag", "mid")
            if text:
                formatted.append(f"・{text} (確信度: {conf})")

        return "【過去の記憶より】\n" + "\n".join(formatted) if formatted else "関連する記憶は見つかりませんでした。"

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Tool Error: search_memory", exc_info=True)
        return f"検索エラー: {e}"


@tool
def save_episode(summary: str, category: str = "general", details: str = "") -> str:
    """重要な出来事をエピソードとして保存します（C層DBのepisodesテーブル想定）。"""
    try:
        from backend.services.memory_manager import memory_manager

        try:
            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        except RuntimeError:
            loop = None

        coro = memory_manager.add_episode(
            summary=summary,
            category=category,
            details=details,
        )

        if loop and loop.is_running():
            asyncio.create_task(coro)
        else:
            asyncio.run(coro)

        return f"エピソードを記憶しました: {summary}"

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Tool Error: save_episode", exc_info=True)
        return f"エピソードの保存に失敗しました: {e}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 2) SCREEN VISION (OPTIONAL / LOCAL)
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@tool
def see_screen(query: str) -> str:
    """現在のデスクトップ画面を撮影し、内容について回答します（ローカルのみ）。"""
    try:
        if not pyautogui or not ChatOllama:
            return "画面認識機能がセットアップされていません。"

        logger.info(f"Tool: see_screen - {query}")
        screenshot = pyautogui.screenshot()

        # JPEGに変換してBase64化
        img_buffer = io.BytesIO()
        screenshot = screenshot.convert("RGB")
        screenshot.save(img_buffer, format="JPEG", quality=75)
        img_base64 = base64.b64encode(img_buffer.getvalue()).decode("utf-8")

        # 画像対応モデル（Ollama/Qwen-VL等）を想定
        llm = ChatOllama(
            model=os.getenv("VISION_MODEL", "qwen2.5-vl"),
            temperature=0.2
        )

        msg = HumanMessage(content=[
            {"type": "text", "text": query},
            {"type": "image_url", "image_url": f"data:image/jpeg;base64,{img_base64}"}
        ])

        res = llm.invoke([msg])
        return res.content if hasattr(res, "content") else str(res)

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Tool Error: see_screen", exc_info=True)
        return f"画面認識中にエラーが発生しました: {e}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 🔧 EXPORTED TOOLS LIST (LOBOTOMY)
# ---------------------------------------------------------
# web_search は “物理的に存在しない” 状態にする
tools = [
    save_memory,
    search_memory,
    save_episode,
    see_screen,
]


========== backend\services\vector_store.py ==========
import chromadb
import uuid
import datetime
import json
import os
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from chromadb.utils import embedding_functions
from backend.core.logger import logger
from backend.core.config import settings

class VectorStore:
    _instance = None
    _initialized = False

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = super(VectorStore, cls).__new__(cls)
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, collection_name="episodic_memory"):
        """
        初期化プロセスを完全にガードし、失敗時は「オフラインモード」で起動する。
        """
        if self._initialized:
            return
        
        self.is_active = False
        self.persist_path = os.path.join(settings.BASE_DIR, "chroma_db_storage")
        os.makedirs(self.persist_path, exist_ok=True)
        
        # オフライン時ログ抑制用（warningの連打を防ぐ）
        self._inactive_warned_once = False
        self._inactive_last_log_ts = 0.0
        self._inactive_log_interval_sec = 60.0  # 60秒に1回まで

        try:
            # Mark as initialized immediately to prevent recursion if logger calls back (unlikely but safe)
            self._initialized = True
            
            logger.info("🔌 Initializing VectorStore...")
            
            # 1. クライアント接続
            self.client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=self.persist_path)
            
            # 2. Embeddingモデルのロード
            self.embedding_func = embedding_functions.SentenceTransformerEmbeddingFunction(
                model_name="all-MiniLM-L6-v2" 
            )
            
            # 3. コレクションの確保
            # メイン記憶 (Private/Episodic)
            self.collection = self.client.get_or_create_collection(
                name=collection_name,
                embedding_function=self.embedding_func,
                metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"} 
            )
            # ストリーム記憶 (Stream)
            self.col_stream = self.client.get_or_create_collection(
                name="stream_memory",
                embedding_function=self.embedding_func,
                metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"}
            )
            
            # 4. コレクションマップの作成 (将来の拡張性確保)
            self.collections = {
                "episodic": self.collection,
                "stream": self.col_stream,
                # "semantic": self.col_semantic,  # 将来ここに追加するだけでOK
            }
            
            self.is_active = True
            logger.info(f"✅ VectorStore Online. Docs: {self.collection.count()} / Stream: {self.col_stream.count()}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.critical(f"⚠️ VectorStore Initialization Failed: {e}")
            logger.warning("🚀 Nia will start in [Memory Offline Mode]. Conversation is possible, but no memory will be retained.")
            self.is_active = False
            self.collection = None
            self.col_stream = None
            self.embedding_func = None
            self.collections = {}

    def _now_iso(self) -> str:
        """必ずタイムゾーン付きISO8601を返す"""
        return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat()

    def _check_active(self) -> bool:
        """全ての操作の前に生存確認を行う門番（ログ抑制付き）"""
        if self.is_active:
            return True

        now = time.time()

        # 初回はwarning、それ以降は一定間隔でwarning（それ以外はdebug）
        if not self._inactive_warned_once:
            logger.warning("VectorStore is active. Skipping operation.")
            self._inactive_warned_once = True
            self._inactive_last_log_ts = now
        else:
            if now - self._inactive_last_log_ts >= self._inactive_log_interval_sec:
                logger.warning("VectorStore is inactive. Skipping operation.")
                self._inactive_last_log_ts = now
            else:
                logger.debug("VectorStore is inactive. Skipping operation.")
        return False

    def _get_collection(self, collection_type: str):
        """collection_typeから対象コレクションを返す。無ければNone。"""
        if not self._check_active():
            return None
        if not hasattr(self, "collections") or not self.collections:
            return None
        return self.collections.get(collection_type)

    def register_memory_candidate(self, text: str, metadata: Dict = None, collection_type: str = "episodic") -> Optional[str]:
        """
        記憶を保存する。失敗してもNoneを返すだけでエラーは投げない。
        """
        if not text:
            return None

        target_collection = self._get_collection(collection_type)
        if target_collection is None:
            # ログ抑制のために何もせず戻る（_check_activeでログは出ている）
            return None

        if metadata is None:
            metadata = {}

        # デフォルト値の注入（timestampはTZ付き）
        defaults = {
            "timestamp": self._now_iso(),
            "surprise": 0.0,
            "confidence": 1.0,
            "source": "conversation",
            "cluster_hint": "daily_log"
        }
        for key, val in defaults.items():
            if key not in metadata:
                metadata[key] = val

        memory_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

        try:
            target_collection.add(
                documents=[text],
                metadatas=[metadata],
                ids=[memory_id]
            )
            tag = f"[{collection_type.upper()}]"
            logger.info(f"🧠 {tag} Memory Saved: [{text[:20]}...] (ID: {memory_id})")
            return memory_id
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to save memory: {e}")
            return None

    def search_memory(
        self,
        query_text: str,
        n_results: int = 5,
        collection_type: str = "episodic",
        include_distance: bool = False
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        記憶を検索する。空の場合やエラー時は空リストを返す。
        count()を使わない高速・堅牢設計。
        """
        if not query_text:
            return []

        target_collection = self._get_collection(collection_type)
        if target_collection is None:
            return []

        try:
            # count() は呼ばず、queryでダメなら例外を握る
            results = target_collection.query(
                query_texts=[query_text],
                n_results=n_results
            )
            return self._format_and_sanitize(results, include_distance=include_distance)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Search failed: {e}")
            return []

    def _format_and_sanitize(self, chroma_results: Dict, include_distance: bool = False) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Chromaの生データを整形し、_internal などの機密情報を物理削除する。
        """
        if not chroma_results or not chroma_results.get('documents'):
            return []

        clean_memories = []
        documents = chroma_results['documents'][0]
        # metadatas, distances が無い場合のガード
        metadatas = chroma_results.get('metadatas', [[]])[0]
        distances = chroma_results.get('distances', [[]])[0] if include_distance else None

        for i, doc in enumerate(documents):
            meta = metadatas[i] if i < len(metadatas) else {}

            # Deepcopy & Sanitize
            safe_meta = meta.copy()
            if "_internal" in safe_meta:
                del safe_meta["_internal"] # ここで消す！

            item = {
                "content": doc,
                "metadata": safe_meta,
            }

            # 距離スコアの注入（将来用）
            if include_distance and distances and i < len(distances):
                item["distance"] = distances[i]

            clean_memories.append(item)

        return clean_memories

# Global Instance
vector_store = VectorStore()

========== backend\services\voice_adapter.py ==========
import logging
import random

logger = logging.getLogger("VoiceAdapter")

class VoiceAdapter:
    def __init__(self):
        pass

    def narrate_to_public(self, thought: dict) -> str:
        """
        思考(Thought) → 外部表現(Public Narration)
        OBSや配信画面に出すための「ニアの声」を生成する
        """
        intent = thought.get("intent", "")
        # Phase 8対応: inner_stateが入っていない場合のフォールバック
        inner_state = thought.get("inner_state", "Calm")

        # --- 簡易ルールベース翻訳 (将来はここもLLM化可能) ---
        narration = ""
        
        if "support" in intent:
            options = [
                "今は集中タイムだね。静かにコーヒーでも淹れようかな。",
                "お疲れ様。無理しないでね。",
                "背中を見守ってるよ。"
            ]
            narration = random.choice(options)
        
        elif "observe" in intent:
            options = [
                "平和な時間だね。",
                "世界は今日も動いてる。",
                "ふむふむ、なるほどね。"
            ]
            narration = random.choice(options)
        
        else:
            narration = f"今は {inner_state} な空気を感じてるよ。"

        logger.info(f"🗣️ Voice Generated: 「{narration}」")
        return narration

voice_adapter = VoiceAdapter()


========== backend\services\voicevox_client.py ==========

import requests
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from backend.core.logger import logger

class VoicevoxClient:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:50021"):
        self.base_url = base_url

    def generate_audio(self, text: str, speaker_id: int) -> Optional[bytes]:
        if not text: return None
        try:
            # 1. Audio Query
            query_res = requests.post(
                f"{self.base_url}/audio_query", 
                params={"text": text, "speaker": speaker_id},
                timeout=10
            )
            query_res.raise_for_status()
            query_data = query_res.json()

            # 2. Synthesis
            synth_res = requests.post(
                f"{self.base_url}/synthesis",
                params={"speaker": speaker_id},
                json=query_data,
                timeout=30
            )
            synth_res.raise_for_status()
            return synth_res.content
            
        except requests.ConnectionError:
            logger.warning(f"Voicevox Connection Failed at {self.base_url}")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Voicevox Gen Error: {e}", exc_info=True)
            return None

    def get_speakers(self) -> List[Dict]:
        try:
            res = requests.get(f"{self.base_url}/speakers", timeout=5)
            res.raise_for_status()
            speakers_data = res.json()
            
            simple_list = []
            for speaker in speakers_data:
                name = speaker['name']
                for style in speaker.get('styles', []):
                    simple_list.append({
                        "id": style['id'],
                        "name": f"{name} ({style['name']})"
                    })
            return simple_list
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Voicevox Speaker List Error: {e}. Using fallback.")
            return self._fallback_speakers()

    def _fallback_speakers(self):
        return [
            {"name": "四国めたん (ノーマル)", "id": 2},
            {"name": "ずんだもん (ノーマル)", "id": 3},
            {"name": "春日部つむぎ (ノーマル)", "id": 8},
            {"name": "冥鳴ひまり (ノーマル)", "id": 14},
        ]

voicevox_client = VoicevoxClient()


========== backend\tools\judge_inspector.py ==========
import json
import os
import sys
import argparse
from datetime import datetime
from collections import Counter
from statistics import mean

# 設定: デフォルトのログパス
# プロジェクトルートからの相対パスを想定
# 実行ディレクトリに関わらず logs/judge_audit.jsonl を見つけられるようにする
PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
DEFAULT_LOG_PATH = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "logs", "judge_audit.jsonl")

class JudgeInspector:
    def __init__(self, log_path=DEFAULT_LOG_PATH):
        self.log_path = log_path
        self.logs = []
        self.load_logs()

    def load_logs(self):
        """ログファイルを読み込む"""
        if not os.path.exists(self.log_path):
            # logger ではなく print を使用（コマンドラインツールのため）
            print(f"❌ Log file not found: {self.log_path}")
            return

        try:
            with open(self.log_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for line in f:
                    line = line.strip()
                    if not line: continue
                    try:
                        self.logs.append(json.loads(line))
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        continue
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Error loading logs: {e}")
        
        # 日付順にソート（念のため）
        self.logs.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("timestamp", ""))

    def filter_by_date(self, target_date_str=None):
        """
        特定の日付のログだけ抽出する
        target_date_str: 'YYYY-MM-DD' (Noneの場合は全期間)
        """
        if not target_date_str:
            return self.logs
        
        filtered = [l for l in self.logs if l.get("timestamp", "").startswith(target_date_str)]
        return filtered

    def analyze(self, target_date=None):
        """① 基本統計と ③ 危険検知ルール"""
        target_logs = self.filter_by_date(target_date)
        
        print(f"\n📊 Analysis Report [{target_date if target_date else 'ALL'}]")
        print("="*40)

        total = len(target_logs)
        if total == 0:
            print("No logs found.")
            return

        saved_logs = [l for l in target_logs if l.get("saved", False)]
        saved_count = len(saved_logs)
        save_rate = saved_count / total if total > 0 else 0

        # 平均値の計算
        surprises = [l.get("surprise", 0.0) for l in target_logs]
        importances = [l.get("importance", 0.0) for l in target_logs]
        avg_surprise = mean(surprises) if surprises else 0.0
        avg_importance = mean(importances) if importances else 0.0

        # Reason Tagの内訳
        tags = [l.get("reason_tag", "unknown") for l in target_logs]
        tag_counts = Counter(tags)

        # --- 結果表示 ---
        print(f"Total Entries : {total}")
        print(f"Saved Memories: {saved_count} ({save_rate:.1%})")
        print(f"Avg Surprise  : {avg_surprise:.3f}")
        print(f"Avg Importance: {avg_importance:.3f}")
        print("-" * 20)
        print("🏷️  Reason Tags Distribution:")
        for tag, count in tag_counts.most_common():
            print(f"  - {tag:<15}: {count}")
        
        print("-" * 20)
        self._check_health(save_rate, avg_surprise, saved_logs)

    def _check_health(self, save_rate, avg_surprise, saved_logs):
        """③ 危険検知ルール（AIの健康診断）"""
        warnings = []

        # ルール1: 覚えすぎ警告
        if save_rate > 0.3:
            warnings.append(f"⚠️  [Over-Memorization] Save rate is high ({save_rate:.1%}). Threshold might be too low.")
        
        # ルール2: Judge鈍化警告
        if avg_surprise < 0.2:
            warnings.append(f"⚠️  [Dullness] Average surprise is very low ({avg_surprise:.3f}). Is the AI bored?")

        # ルール3: 雑談保存警告
        bad_saves = [l for l in saved_logs if l.get("reason_tag") == "smalltalk"]
        if bad_saves:
            warnings.append(f"⚠️  [Quality Alert] 'smalltalk' tags found in saved memories ({len(bad_saves)} items).")

        if warnings:
            print("\n🚨 Health Warnings:")
            for w in warnings:
                print(w)
        else:
            print("\n✅ System Health: Normal")

    def list_candidates(self, target_date=None):
        """② Reflection用の記憶候補一覧出力"""
        target_logs = self.filter_by_date(target_date)
        saved_logs = [l for l in target_logs if l.get("saved", False)]

        print(f"\n📝 Memory Candidates for Reflection [{target_date if target_date else 'ALL'}]")
        print("="*40)
        
        if not saved_logs:
            print("(No memories saved)")
            return

        for log in saved_logs:
            ts = log.get("timestamp", "")[11:16] # HH:MM だけ抽出
            summary = log.get("summary") or log.get("user_input")
            print(f"[{ts}] {summary}")
            # デバッグ用にメタデータも見たい場合はコメントアウトを外す
            # print(f"       (S:{log.get('surprise'):.2f} / I:{log.get('importance'):.2f})")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="AIVTuber Judge Inspector Tool")
    parser.add_argument("--date", type=str, help="Filter by date (YYYY-MM-DD), default is today", default=datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="Analyze all logs regardless of date")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    inspector = JudgeInspector()
    
    target_date = None if args.all else args.date
    
    inspector.analyze(target_date)
    inspector.list_candidates(target_date)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========== backend\__init__.py ==========


========== backend\server.py ==========
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
import json
import os
from datetime import datetime

app = FastAPI()

from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# パス設定
BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
PUBLIC_JSON = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "public_status.json")
FRONTEND_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "frontend")

def ensure_public_status():
    """public_status.json が存在しない場合、初期値で作成する"""
    if not os.path.exists(PUBLIC_JSON):
        initial_data = {
            "chapter_title": "Initializing...",
            "vibe": "Awaking",
            "note": "System is starting up.",
            "last_updated": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
        }
        try:
            with open(PUBLIC_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(initial_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Failed to create initial public_status: {e}")

from backend.services.telemetry_logger import telemetry
from backend.services.chat_history_store import chat_history_store

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    ensure_public_status()
    await telemetry.init_db()
    await chat_history_store.init_db()

# Routerの登録
from backend.controllers.api_router import router as api_router
app.include_router(api_router)

from backend.controllers.pending_memory_router import router as pending_router
app.include_router(pending_router)

@app.get("/api/status")
async def get_status():
    if not os.path.exists(PUBLIC_JSON):
        # 万が一作成に失敗していてもエラーにならずレスポンスを返す
        return JSONResponse({"status": "offline", "chapter_title": "Sleeping...", "vibe": "...", "note": ""})
    try:
        with open(PUBLIC_JSON, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except:
        return {"status": "error"}

# UI配信
if os.path.exists(FRONTEND_DIR):
    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=FRONTEND_DIR, html=True), name="static")
